台新證券投資顧問股份有限公司 台北市仁愛路四段 118 號 16 樓(02)5589-9558  (113) 金管投顧新字第 021 號 
公司拜訪快報

南亞(1303)：電子材料獲利與業外轉投資貢獻優於預期 
建議：維持買進

一、3Q26 自結獲利優於預期 
3Q26 營收季增 14.3%至 955.97 億元， 加上電子材料獲
利大幅成長，以及業外南亞科及台塑化挹注，單季自結
EPS 達 4.90 元、優於預期；累計前三季 EPS 為 10.07
元。各部門營益率中，電子材料自 2Q26 的 19.9%跳升
至 30.9%，塑膠加工則為 5.4%、化工部門 0.6%、聚酯
部門 0.2%。 化工與聚酯部門獲利衰退主要是2Q26 有低
價庫存，塑膠加工則因通膨干擾終端消費。然而，EG
在中國華東港口庫存嚴重偏低下，近一季報價及利差表
現好轉，抵銷可塑劑產品與 BPA 的虧損，聚酯部門則有
美國外銷動能挹注，支撐兩個部門 3Q26 小幅獲利。展
望 4Q26， 終端消費依舊沒有明顯的動能 ， 但EG 報價及
利差持穩且聚酯朝電子應用發展，管理層預期化工、聚
酯部門營益率與 3Q26 相當，電子材料在漲價延續及產
品組合優化下，營利率有望持續走升。

二、2027 年營運動能依舊為電子材料與南亞科貢獻 
3Q26 電子材料營收達 555 億元，其中，CCL 營收比重
達 31%、PCB(南電) 29%， 銅箔與Epoxy 則各佔 11%、
玻纖 7%。 獲利結構來看 ， 玻纖營益率走升至40%以上，
CCL 則季增 10 個百分點至 30%並高於 PCB 的 26%，
銅箔受惠加工費調漲達 15%，Epoxy 則維持 10~11%左
右。2027 年電子材料依舊是成長主力，部門營收比重將
達 60%以上，營益率有望在單季接近 40%的水準，但材
料漲價趨勢會從玻纖轉為 CCL。轉投資方面，南亞科因
記憶體市場供需缺口急遽放大 ， 預估將貢獻2026、2027
年稅前 EPS 9.17 與 16.74 元。

三、高階 CCL 訂單自 2H26 逐步出貨，2027 年效益將
明顯放大 
2025 年南亞 CCL 單價約每張 500 元，3Q26 已走升至
1,000~1,100 元/張，主要受惠漲價與產品組合優化。管
理層預期 4Q26 維持每月漲福 5~10%，且有機會延續至
1Q27。3Q26 中下旬 M 系列在 CCL 的營收比重由 20%
提升至 30%，漲價及產品組合優化都有影響，1Q27 在
M6、M7 訂單放量下會有更明顯的增長。目前 M4 CCL
單價約 1,100~1,200 元/張，M6 約當每張 2,200~2,300
元，M7 單價則跳升至 4,000 元以上 ， 有助2027 年 ASP
漲幅進一步擴大。此外，3Q26 的 CCL 產能利用率達
90~95%，現階段 CCL 月產能 618 萬張，規劃樹林、新
港將進行產線整改，預計 2026 年底跟明年可以各增加
約 15 萬張/月。 
四、維持買進理由 
3Q26 自結獲利優於預期，電子材料漲價與 CCL 產品組
合優化支撐本業中長期動能，轉投資南亞科(2408)與台
塑化(6505)大幅挹注業外收益 ， 上修2026、2027 年 EPS
分別為 15.72 及 29.24 元。考量獲利顯著成長有助股息
提高，且市場因公司獲利結構轉型而 Re-rating，維持買
進評等，目標價調升至 450 元(20x 2027 本業 EPS+10x 
2027 南亞科 EPS)。

2026. 10.27
先週（10/19～10/26）発表の主な経済指標
米生産活動は堅調。AI関連投資を背景にコンピューター・電子
製品や機械などがけん引。一方、データセンター建設に伴う需要
増加が、他産業の生産や建設活動を圧迫する兆候も
コア消費者物価は再び2％超え。特殊要因を除くサービス価格
も加速し、人件費転嫁の兆し。連合は2027年春闘で6％中心
を要求。中小企業の余力に課題も、物価上振れに注意
注目点② サービス物価の加速で重み増す2027年春闘戦線注目点①AI投資の恩恵拡大、副作用もじわり顕在化
総合PMIは57.6と高水準を維持。新規失業保険申
請件数も19.4万件と低水準で解雇の動きは引き続
き限定的。一方で、住宅着工件数や住宅建設業
者の景況感は悪化、高金利の影響が継続

コメント要旨出典
耐久財製造業は、軍需やデータセンター（DC）増
設の旺盛な需要が、他の製品の生産を圧迫して
いると報告ベージュブック
（2026/10） テクノロジー企業がDC向け電気設備の買い占め
を試みたが、供給業者は他案件への供給を優先
し、これを拒否
機械製造業は、DC向けに鋼材在庫が大量に消
費されるなか、鉄鋼メーカーによる供給制限が他
の建設案件の遅延を招いていると指摘
テキサス州
見通し調査
（2026/9）
製造業では原材料コストが上昇。特に鋼材価格
が急上昇しており、その一因はDC需要とされるベージュブック
（2026/9） 一部の製造業がアルミニウム・銅・鋼材の供給不
足や調達期間の長期化を報告
一部の自動車メーカーはアルミニウムや一部部品
の供給不足で生産が幾分抑制されたと報告

ベージュブック
（2026/7）
米国：AI関連を中心に製造業生産は堅調も、資材・設備の需給ひっ迫が他産業に波及
 米国の製造業生産は、AI関連投資を背景にコンピューター・電子製品、一次金属、一般機械などが堅調
― 一方、非耐久財や家具など消費関連の生産は軟調に推移し、業種間のばらつきが拡大
 FRBは、データセンター建設に伴う鋼材・電気設備などの需要増加が、他の建設案件や生産活動に影響していると指摘
―AI投資の拡大が関連産業の生産を押し上げる一方、供給制約を通じて他産業の活動を圧迫する兆候も
― 当面はAI投資による成長押し上げ効果が期待される一方、供給制約や価格上昇を通じた負の影響にも注意が必要
4

―2026年冬の国際線旅客便数の回復ペースは緩慢。中国路線の回復は見込みにくく、目先の訪日外客数は伸び悩みへ

― 原油高が長期化すれば航空運賃が高止まりし、欧米中心に訪日外客数を一段と下押しするリスクに
緩やかながら増勢は維持

### Page 2

2  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/
上週重要市場資訊
 澳洲 8 月就業人數勁增 3.95 萬人大幅優於預期，但勞動參與率上升至 67.1%，
推升失業率至 4.6%，呈現勞動力擴增但市場邊際降溫跡象。澳洲央行總裁布拉
克日前指出，失業率回升至5%有助於抑制頑固通膨。考量物價長期高於目標中
點，市場預期澳洲央行本週二將重啟緊縮，升息 1 碼至 4.60%，創 2011年底以
來新高。
 瑞士央行（SNB）上週四宣布連續第五次將關鍵政策利率維持在0%不變，符合
市場預期。儘管受油價推升使 8 月通膨微升至 0.8%，但仍處於目標區間，央行
預期中期通膨壓力溫和，現行極度寬鬆立場依然適當。決議公布後瑞郎兌美元跌
至 1.2077。相較全球主要央行相繼緊縮，瑞士央行維持超低利率，策略轉為視情
況調節匯市以穩定瑞郎。
 標普全球公布歐元區 9 月綜合 PMI 初值升至 53.1，創三年新高，製造與服務業
同步擴張，新訂單增速達四年多最快，印證經濟無懼美伊衝突引發的能源成本飆

升，展現超預期韌性。受能源價格傳導加速影響，通膨壓力再度升溫，儘管尚未
浮現薪資二輪效應，但強勁景氣數據促使市場大幅提高歐洲央行提前於10月例
會再度升息的押注。
 美國 9 月綜合 PMI 初值勁升至 58.4，創 62 個月新高；製造業與服務業 PMI 亦
逆勢走強至 57與 58.7，凸顯企業擴張強勁，引發市場對通膨持續性的擔憂。市
場焦點轉向 9 月 30日公布的美國 8 月 PCE、個人消費支出與 Q2 GDP終值，同
日日本亦將發布短觀調查與央行意見摘要。若終端消費延續強勁，將牽動美日央
行政策利率預期。

### Page 3

3  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
 美元兌新台幣匯率走勢  
Sep/29/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
上週台幣匯價高點在 31.655，低點在 31.866，以 31.790作收，較前一周收盤價
升值 2.8分。 
上週台幣先升後貶，周初，台股創新高，外資連續五日買超台股並匯入，推升
台幣最高來到 31.655，不過逢低吸引進口商、政府基金等買盤進場，台幣主要在
31.700 附近震盪；臨近周末，美國 9 月綜合 PMI 由 8 月的 56 走升至 58.4，創下逾
5 年來的新高，加上美伊戰事持續，皆推升市場對後續升息的預期，美元指數升至
101 上方，台幣跳空開低，回貶至 31.800 上方，最低來到 31.866，不過隨後出口商
賣壓出籠，台幣跌勢放緩，最終在央行調節之下收在 31.790。 
＜未來分析＞ 
     預估本週台幣區間震盪。就國際市場而言，美國經濟表現仍具韌性，9月綜合
PMI 由 8 月的 56 上升至 58.4，企業活動加速至逾 5 年來最強，數據公布後美國 10
年債券殖利率升破 5%，Fed Watch 顯示市場對於聯準會 10 月升息預期升高，有助
於美元指數維持偏強格局，預計將給台幣帶來壓力；就台灣市場來看，台股在創下
新高後回落，而外資也結束連續五日買超的走勢，轉為賣超，短線上資金匯入不利
台幣表現，然而，台灣8 月外銷訂單達 1,029.6億美元、年增 71.4%，創歷史新高，
AI 需求帶動出口持續強勁，出口商後續拋匯需求預計將提供台幣支撐。綜合以上，
預估本週台幣區間震盪。 
＜預估本週＞ 美元兌台幣：31.600-32.000

### Page 4

4  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
美元兌人民幣匯率走勢  
Sep/29/2026 
＜上週走勢＞ 
上週人民幣震盪走貶。人民幣中間價在上周連續十天走升，並升破6.7500整數
關口，最高來到 6.7468，帶動人民幣偏升，不過由於美伊戰事持續，市場對於聯準
會升息預期提升，美元指數震盪走強，加上週間公布美國9月綜合 PMI由 8 月的 56
走升至 58.4，創下逾 5 年來的新高，美元指數突破 101 整數關卡，令境外人民幣承
壓，USD/CNH回貶至 6.7200上方，終場境外人民幣收在6.7227，週線貶值283點，
境內人民幣則收在 6.7125，週線貶值 141 點。

＜未來分析＞ 
     預估本週人民幣高檔震盪。就國際市場來看，美中兩國完成新一輪談判，美方
表示雙方同意將原訂 11 月 10 日到期的貿易休戰延長至明年 1 月 10 日，並持續就
關稅、貿易及供應鏈等議題進行協商，降低短期貿易摩擦升溫的風險，對人民幣形
成一定支撐。不過，美國近期經濟數據仍具韌性，加以聯準會偏鷹立場推升美元，
預計將限制人民幣升值空間；就中國國內市場來看，近期人行持續偏強設定中間價，
帶動人民幣維持偏強走勢，惟美中利差再度擴大，預計將限制人民幣升值的空間；
最後就技術面來看，USD/CNH日線圖中，匯價反彈至 20日均線之上，顯示原先下
行動能暫歇，上方壓力先看向季線 6.7350附近，綜合以上，預估本週人民幣高檔震
盪。

＜預估本週＞ 美元兌人民幣：6.6900-6.7400

### Page 5

5  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
美元兌日圓匯率走勢  
         Sep/29/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
上週 USD/JPY先上後下。週初，美國多位聯準會官員鷹派談話，帶動美元指數
持續上行，推升 USD/JPY延續前一週的走勢來到 157上方；週間，受美國 PMI 強
於預期與美債殖利率全面上行帶動，市場重新提高對聯準會後續升息路徑的押注，
帶動 USD/JPY大漲突破 159的關卡；臨近週末，日本首相高市早苗表態日圓被低估
是個問題，使市場提高對政策訊號與潛在干預的敏感度，帶動USD/JPY回吐大部分
漲幅，終場收在 157.26，週線上漲 56點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
  預估本週 USD/JPY區間震盪，主要原因如下：一、日本財務大臣片山皋月上週
五罕見透露，美國總統川普在美日元首峰會上對日圓過度疲弱表達關切，首相高市
早苗亦回應日圓遭低估確屬問題。儘管近期美元指數維持在 101 上方，但在日本政
府可能干預的陰影下，預計將限縮日圓貶值的空間。二、美國9 月綜合 PMI產出指
數創逾五年新高，疊加美伊戰事推升油價突破100美元與柴油大漲，通膨壓力劇增。
美聯儲理事巴爾表態可能需再升息兩次，芝加哥聯儲古爾斯比也示警能源衝擊具持
續性，推升市場押注 10 月升息機率升至六成，預計將支撐 USD/JPY 走勢。三、就
技術面而言，日線圖中，USD/JPY連日上漲一度站回年線但隨即失守，目前依舊承
壓於季線及年線下方，不過各天期均線趨平，且下方尚有月線支撐，短線尚無明確
的方向。綜合以上，預估 USD/JPY本週呈現區間震盪的可能性較高。 
＜預估本週＞ 美元兌日幣：155.30-159.30

### Page 6

6  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
美元兌歐元匯率走勢  
             Sep/29/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
  上週 EUR/USD 下跌。週初，歐元延續前一週偏弱的走勢，加上聯準會官員鷹
派發言，導致歐元跌破 1.1450的重要關卡；週間，儘管油價自高檔回落，但市場解
讀為能源成本仍處高位、難以迅速回落，推升對美國實質利率與利差優勢的預期。
加上歐洲在能源依賴與成長動能較弱下，相對於美國更難承受長期高利率環境，歐
元最低來到 1.1358；臨近週末，歐元跌深反彈，終場收在1.1391，週線下跌 91點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
  預估本週 EUR/USD 震盪偏弱，主要原因如下：一、歐洲央行首席經濟學家連
恩示警，第二波能源價格飆升將推升通膨，預計至2027年中期始回落至目標水準。
德國央行月報指出，受中東衝突、低天然氣庫存及明年醫療改革影響，通膨將居高
不下；夏季雖因出口疲弱與乾旱致增長趨緩，但在製造業回暖與財政支出挹注下，
估年底經濟有望重啟復甦，預計將提供歐元一定的支撐。二、美聯儲官員接連釋放
強烈鷹派信號，聖路易聯儲總裁穆薩萊姆直言當前 3.75%-4.00%利率仍偏寬鬆，呼
籲盡早收緊；芝加哥聯儲總裁古爾斯比亦警告，AI投資繁榮引發需求過熱，疊加持
續性供應衝擊，可能迫使美聯儲加速且更積極地升息。目前Fedwatch顯示預期明年
4 月前可能再升息三次，將不利歐元表現。三、技術面而言，在日線圖中，EUR/USD
在各天期均線下方，且接連失守1.15及 1.14整數關卡，空方動能較佔優勢。綜合以
上，預估 EUR/USD本週呈現震盪偏弱的可能性較高。

2026 年 10 月 23 日 全6 頁

Indicators Update 
2026 年 9 月全国消費者物価 
サービスや非耐久財で価格上昇、コアCPI は9 カ月ぶり＋2％台

経済調査部 エコノミスト 横田 凱

[要約] 
◼ 2026 年 9 月の全国コアCPI（除く生鮮食品）は前年比＋2.1％と前月から伸び率が拡大
し、9 カ月ぶりに 2％台となった。生鮮食品やエネルギーを除いた全国新コアコア CPI
は同＋2.4％と、こちらも前月から伸び率が拡大し、6 カ月ぶりに 2％台に達した。食
料品、旅行関連、紙製品などの値上げと、政策要因の剥落が重なり、サービスや非耐
久消費財（除く生鮮食品、エネルギー）の伸び率が高まった。 
◼ コア CPI の前年比の動きを財・サービス別に見ると、サービス、非耐久消費財（除く
生鮮食品、エネルギー） 、耐久消費財、半耐久消費の伸び率が拡大し、エネルギーのマ
イナス幅が縮小した。 
◼ 先行きの物価上昇率について、コア CPI 上昇率は 26 年度末にかけて前年比＋3％超ま
で高まると見込んでいる。原油高が、エネルギーにとどまらず、幅広い財・サービス
の価格へ波及するとみられる。円安や、賃上げの価格転嫁と併せ、基調的な物価への
上昇圧力となるだろう。27 年度は、こうした物価上昇圧力は継続する一方で、飲食料
品にかかる消費税率の引き下げにより、コアCPI の上昇率は同＋1％台に縮小するとみ
られる。

### Page 2

1

2 / 6

26 年9 月 CPI：食料品の値上げや政策要因の剥落でコア CPI の伸び率は拡大 
2026 年9 月の全国コアCPI（除く生鮮食品）は前年比＋2.1％と前月から0.4％pt 伸び率が拡
大し、9 カ月ぶりに 2％台となった。また、全国新コアコア CPI（除く生鮮食品、エネルギー）
（同＋2.4％）も前月から0.5％pt 伸び率が拡大し、6 カ月ぶりに2％台に達した（図表1）。 
食料品、旅行関連、紙製品などの価格上昇と、政策要因の剥落が重なって、サービスや非耐
久消費財（除く生鮮食品、エネルギー）の伸び率が高まった。 
食料品については、コメ価格は下落基調にある一方で、他の品目で価格が上昇しており、生
鮮食品除く食料は前年比＋2.9％（8 月：同＋2.7％）と、14 カ月ぶりに伸び率が拡大した。旅
行関連は、大型連休が25 年9 月より長かったため需要が強く、価格を押し上げたとみられる。 
また、東京都の政策要因の剥落が全国の指数にも影響した。25 年 9 月に保育料無償化を拡充
したことの裏の影響や、水道料減免が終了したことで、コア指数の前年比が 0.1％pt 程度押し
上げられた。

食料品などの値上げや政策要因の剥落で、サービスや非耐久財が上昇 
コア CPI の前年比の動きを財・サービス別に見ると（図表 3）、サービス（前年比＋1.5％） 、
非耐久消費財（除く生鮮食品、エネルギー） （同＋3.4％） 、耐久消費財（同＋3.8％） 、半耐久
消費財（同＋3.1％）の伸び率が拡大し、エネルギー（同▲0.6％）のマイナス幅が縮小した。 
サービスでは、宿泊料（8 月：前年比▲1.4％→9 月：同＋4.6％）の伸び率がプラスに転じた
ほか、外国パック旅行費（8 月：同＋9.7％→9 月：同＋23.5％）のプラス幅が拡大した。これ
ら 2 品目だけでコア指数の前年比を 0.1％pt 程度押し上げた。また、保育所保育料（8 月：同
▲21.6％→9 月：同▲13.3％）のマイナス幅が縮小した。東京都が 25 年 9 月に無償化を拡充し
たことの裏の影響が出た。 
非耐久消費財（除く生鮮食品、エネルギー）では、水道料（8 月：前年比▲3.5％→9 月：同
＋4.9％）の伸び率がプラスに転じた 。東京都の水道料減免が終了したためだ。 食料品では、
冷凍ぎょうざ（8 月：同▲6.5％→9 月：同▲1.3％）などのマイナス幅が縮小した。また、トイ
レットペーパー（8 月：同＋10.5％→9 月：同＋17.8％）など、紙製品の伸び率が拡大した。 
耐久消費財では、冷蔵庫（8 月：前年比▲1.8％→9 月：同＋2.6％） 、パソコン（ノート型）
（8 月：同▲3.2％→9 月：同＋0.8％）などの伸び率がプラスに転じたほか、テレビ（8 月：同
＋10.7％→9 月：同＋14.8％）のプラス幅が拡大した。半導体価格高騰の影響が続いている。 
半耐久消費財では、ゴルフクラブ（8 月：前年比＋11.3％→9 月：同＋43.3％） 、自動車バッ
テリー（8 月：同＋1.4％→9 月：同＋7.0％） 、自動車タイヤ（8 月：同＋0.1％→9 月：同＋
1.4％）などの伸び率が拡大した。 
エネルギーでは、都市ガス代（8 月：前年比▲0.8％→9 月：同＋0.2％）の伸び率がプラスに
転じた。政府による補助金額は増加したが、資源高の影響を相殺するには至らなかった。電気
代やガソリンの前年比は前月から横ばいだった。

先行き：原油高などの波及で、コア CPI は26 年度末にかけて＋3％超を予想 
先行きの物価上昇率について、コア CPI 上昇率は 26 年度末にかけて前年比＋3％超まで高ま
るとみている。当面、原油高の幅広い財・サービスへの波及が物価を押し上げる主因となるだ
ろう。また、円安や、賃上げの価格転嫁も物価上昇につながるとみられる。 
原油価格の動向には注意が必要だ。米国とイスラエルが、米中間選挙後にイランへの攻撃を
再開する計画を策定したと伝えられる2など、中東の和平に向けた先行きは未だ不透明である。
原油価格の代表的な指標であるWTI 先物価格は 10 月 22 日終値で 99 ドル/バレル程度と、中東
情勢の悪化前よりも大幅に高い状況が続いている。さらに、代替調達の進展などを受け、輸入
原油価格は上昇が続くとみられる3。 
こうした原油高は、直接的にエネルギー価格を押し上げる。電気・ガスの燃料費調整制度で
は、3-5 カ月前のエネルギー資源の平均輸入価格が料金に反映されるため、原油高がラグを伴
って波及する。関連して、政府は9 月使用分（10 月請求分）まで電気・ガス料金の補助を実施
しているほか、27 年1-3 月にも同様の措置を検討していると報じられている4。しかし、25 年
夏、26 年冬にも同様の政策を実施しているため、前年比でみた物価上昇率の抑制効果は限定的
なものとなるだろう（巻末図表「エネルギー価格のコアCPI への影響とその見通し」）。また、
原油高は、エネルギー価格だけでなく、輸送費や原材料費の増加を通じて幅広い品目の価格に
も波及するため5、基調的な物価への上昇圧力となるとみられる。 
併せて、ドル円レートは足元で150 円/ドル台後半であり、前年同期比では円安水準にあ
る。原油価格の上昇も相まって輸入物価への押し上げ圧力が強い状態であり、消費者物価への
波及が見込まれる。 
また、賃上げによる人件費の増加分を販売価格に転嫁する動きも継続するだろう。26 年度春
闘における定期昇給（定昇）相当込みの賃上げ率は、加重平均で 5.01％と高水準を維持してい
る6。さらに、26 年度の最低賃金の全国加重平均額も前年度比 5％程度引き上げられる7。こう
した賃金上昇は、サービス価格を中心に物価を押し上げると見込んでいる。 
これらの物価上昇圧力は27 年度にかけても継続するだろう。他方、政府は飲食料品にかか
る消費税率を27 年4 月から2 年間1％に引き下げる方針である。引き下げが実施されれば、27
年度のコアCPI 上昇率は1％pt 超押し下げられ、前年比＋1％台まで縮小するだろう。

Document 131: 261027_daiwa_faraday

Source PDF: all report pdf/261027_daiwa_faraday.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-10-27
Pages: 7
Status: ok

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

What’s new: Faraday released a mixed set of results and guidance on 27 
October. 3Q26 core results missed consensus on an unfavourable product 
mix and higher opex, while 4Q26 guidance was broadly intact on delayed 
MCU business rebalancing. Regardless of the near-term hiccup, 
management expects its 3 product lines of mass-production (MP), IP and 
non-recurrent engineering (NRE) to fire on all cylinders into 2027 for a 
double-digit % YoY revenue growth. This concurs with our turnaround story 
for the counter, and we reiterate our Buy (1) rating and 12M TP of TWD220.

What’s the impact: Results mixed, guidance intact. Faraday’s 3Q26 net 
profit came in at TWD279m, 9% above our estimate and beating 
consensus by a meaningful 38%. Despite the beat, dynamics were mixed 
(table on p.2). Core operations missed despite a higher top line, which was 
spurred by MP on stronger-than-expected MCU sales from its 50%-owned 
Artery (6907 TT, not rated) for a lower gross margin (GM) due to an 
unfavourable revenue mix as MP carries a lower GM than the other 2 lines. 
Higher opex, as a result of salary hikes and employee bonuses, further 
pressured core operations with operating profit missing our/consensus 
estimates by 36%/11%, but non-operating gains from marked-to-market 
investments more than offset the miss. Guidance calls for a roughly flat 
4Q26 top line with a 45% GM, broadly in line with our expectation.

Firing on all cylinders across all 3 lines. In our previous note, we 
expected rebalancing in the MCU business due to demand pull-in on price 
hikes and supply chain uncertainties. It failed to happen in 3Q26 thanks to 
early pull-in of OSAT capacity support, thus pushing back the rebalancing 
to 4Q26 — a key reason why top line was guided to be flat despite the 
other 2 lines of IP and NRE growing QoQ. That said, management expects 
the MCU business to return to a growth track in 2027 after the near-term 
hiccup. Its delayed advanced packaging (AP) ramp on design complexity 
and supply constraint looks set to accelerate in 2027 on OSAT capacity 
improvement which, together with a milestone of 2nm/GAA design-win for a 
cloud ASIC, should add to upside for the NRE and MP business next year. 
All in, we lower our 2026-27E EPS by 9-13% to factor in the higher opex.

What we recommend: Despite our forecast revisions, we keep our 12M 
TP for Faraday unchanged at TWD220 as we maintain our 4-quarter 
forward PER target of 37x. We reaffirm our Buy (1) rating with key 
downside risks of silicon inflation and geopolitical overhangs leading to 
global inflation and prolonging a non-AI recovery.

How we differ: Our 2026-28E EPS are 6-9% below the consensus 
forecasts likely on the street’s lower opex assumptions.

27 October 2026

Turnaround confirmed (II)

➢ 3Q26 results a mixed bag; 4Q26 guidance broadly intact 
➢ Firing on all cylinders into 2027 after near-term hiccups 
➢ Reiterating our Buy (1) call and 12M TP of TWD220

Company profile 
Founded in 1993, Faraday is a hybrid design service provider offering IP and ASIC design services to 
fabless, foundry and system houses. It has R&D and marketing locations in the US, Japan, China, India, 
and Vietnam.

### Page 5

5

Faraday Technology (3035 TT): 27 October 2026 
ESG analysis

Update Date: 10 Feb 2026 
Source: Daiwa, Company

ESG risks
Risks Management Analyst comments
G
Executive/board
quality 2
Faraday’s Board of Directors (BoD) consists of 10 members, including 4 independent directors,
representing 40% of the board—significantly above the regulatory minimum of 20%. Three of the
10 directors are female, reflecting progress toward gender diversity at the board level. The roles
of Chairman and President are separated, supporting stronger governance checks and
balances. However, most non independent directors concurrently serve as executive managers,
which may elevate concerns regarding board independence and management oversight from an
ESG governance perspective.
Capital management 1
Over the past 10 years, Faraday has kept its dividend payout ratio at above 70% most of the
time, in spite of an up or down cycle. As an IC design service company without capex for
manufacturing fabs, Faraday's high dividend payout is appropriate, in our view.
Related party &
transaction 1
UMC, which is Faraday's largest shareholder, is Faraday's major foundry for its turnkey
business. For IC design service companies, a close relationship with foundries is essential since
their design should build on a foundry's process node and their turnkey businesses also require
a foundry's capacity support. UMC's support offers Faraday a competitive advantage, in our
view. Faraday has developed many standard cell and I/O libraries for UMC, covering logic and
mixed-signal specialty processes across 0.5μ to 22nm.
S Supply chainmanagement 1
Faraday has set several evaluation standards for its existing and new suppliers and it requires all
suppliers to get ISO9001 and ISO14001 certifications for quality and environmental
management. In 2024, 100% of its wafer, packaging and testing providers had ISO9001 and
ISO14001 certifications. In addition, Faraday also has on-site audits (quality system and process
quality audit, CSR evaluation and environment management system) for its suppliers with a mid-
to-long term target of conducting annual onsite audits for 100% of its suppliers.
E Water & wastewatermanagement 1
As an IC design service company, Faraday outsources its manufacturing and thus has no own
production sites. As such, Faraday's water use is primarily for general operations, not for
production. Faraday continues to conduct several water reduction measures to manage water
usage.
E
Waste & hazardous
materials
management
1
Faraday has established a green supply chain in accordance with the EU Directive on the
Restriction of the Use of Certain Hazardous Substances in Electrical and Electronic Equipment
(RoHS) and with the EU Chemicals Policy (REACH).
Faraday has a mid-to-long term target of carefully evaluating and selecting raw materials with
low hazard levels to comply with the Hazardous Substance-Free Product Standard. All of its
packaging materials are 100% lead-free and halogen-free.
E GHG emissions 1
With Faraday's energy saving measures, it reduced the GHG emissions intensity gradually. As
an IC design service company without onsite production, Faraday's GHG emissions are much
lower than other semiconductor manufacturing companies. Faraday's low power design also
helps reduce GHG emissions. In 2019, Faraday completed the development of its ultra-low
power consumption 22nm IP solution.
Note: Management score represents a company's ability to manage/benefit from certain ESG topics. The scores range from 1 to 3, with 1 being the strongest.

### Page 1

Faraday

Valuation remains stretched; timely 
revenue delivery to be key watchpoint

Reiterate Rating: UNDERPERFORM | PO: 120.00 TWD | Price: 191.50 TWD

3Q26 sales beat on record MCU mass production revenue  
Faraday’s 3Q26 sales was +11% QoQ, above BofAe/street of -7%/-3% QoQ and its 
guidance of -single-digit% QoQ, mainly driven by strong MP growth (+19.6% QoQ) on 
record-high MCU sales and accelerating ASIC shipments, coupled with continued IP 
revenue strength more than offsetting deferred NRE revenue recognition. 3Q26 GM 
dropped by 270 bps to 42.5% due to product mix, while increased Opex from salary 
adjustments brought 3Q26 EPS to NT$1.07 (BofAe/Street NT$1.27/0.66).  
IP and NRE strength support 4Q26 margin improvement 
4Q26 sales is guided to remain flat QoQ, as growth in IP and NRE revenue offset decline 
in MP revenue on lower MCU shipments following a high base in 3Q26. With sustained 
strength in IP segment underpinned by higher wafer pricing and utilization, along with 
NRE milestone delivery, 4Q26 GM should edge up to 43.7% on better mix, in our view.  
High-end MCU and advanced projects drive 2027 growth  
We now forecast Faraday's 2026/2027 sales to -27%/+36% YoY, supported by healthy 
demand for high-end MCU and gradual ramp in advanced projects. While the design 
change from 4nm to 2nm for a US customer’s ASIC project should bring greater contract 
value, Faraday’s capability to deliver on schedule and achieve timely revenue recognition 
remains key watchpoint. On higher advanced packaging/nodes projects contribution 
through 2027, GM should decline slightly from 44.5% in 2026 to 42.9% in 2027. 
Reiterate Underperform with unchanged NT$120 PO 
We lower our 2026 EPS from NT$4.05 to NT$3.19 on higher Opex and trim 2027-28 
EPS by 3-10% as we remain cautious on the timing of NRE/MP recognition for advanced 
projects given the increased design complexity and constrained OSAT capacity. We 
reiterate Underperform rating with unchanged PO of NT$120 on 17x 2027E P/E. Current 
valuation appears stretched with shares trading at 28x '27 P/E (vs. historical 13-42x).

Company Sector 
Semiconductors

Company Description 
Faraday Technology Corp. was established in 1993 and 
listed in 1999. The company is an affiliate of the second 
biggest semiconductor logic foundry (UMC) in Taiwan, and 
has been the main provider to UMC's IP library. Faraday is 
also known for its total solution in ASIC (customized chips) 
which includes chip-design and turnkey mass production, 
on top of its IP offering. The company has a diversified end-
market exposure, including internet of things, factory 
automation, solar, microcontrollers etc.

Investment Rationale 
We have an Underperform rating on Faraday, as we are 
wary of the slower for longer business outlook in mature 
node projects, while its collaboration with non-TSMC 
foundries bears the uncertainty.

### Page 4

Price objective basis & risk 
Faraday (FDYTF) 
Our PO NT$120 is derived from 17x 2027E P/E, which is at low-to-mid-end of the 
company's historical valuation range. In our view, the multiple is backed by gross margin 
expansion from 27% in 2025 to over 40% in 2026/27 while revenue would slow down 
from a peak in 2025.

Upside risks: (1) faster-than-expected progress in new businesses including advanced 
nodes and advanced packaging, and (2) a better margin profile thanks to more stringent 
opex and enhanced value-add to customers

Downside risks: (1) downturn in foundry business cycle, given that the firm adopts a 
cost-plus model for turnkey (mass production) business, and (2) slower qualification and 
ramp-up of the yield rate for advanced packaging business

Core resilience with CPO upside optionality
Raise PT to NT$1,250 on potential CPO contribution and intact core business
We reiterate Buy and raise our  price target to NT$1,250 from NT$700, rolling valuation
to 2028E and moving from P/B to a P/E-based SOTP that separately values the core lens
business and the emerging co-packaged optics (CPO) business. We believe Genius is
making progress sampling three precision components inside the fiber array unit (FAU)

targeting FAU integrators such as Coherent and FOCI.  We see potential for the company
to qualify the leading FAU module supplier into NVIDIA's CPO program. At the same
time, its core business is outperforming Largan, growing +1% YoY in Q326 vs. -11%
YoY for Largan as its share rises on periscope lens. Compared with the pressure on
Largan's margins from green electricity prices, we believe Genius should maintain its
margin, as its main production site is in Xiamen, whereas Largan's production in Taiwan

is affected by higher electricity prices.
Core intact: periscope share gains offset muted smartphone demand
September sales of NT$2.67bn (−2% MoM, +1% YoY) took Q326 to NT$7.82bn (+38%
QoQ, +1% YoY), in-line with VA consensus. Genius grew 10% YoY in 9M26 against 2%
at Largan, which we attribute to share gains in periscope lenses for premium iPhones,
offsetting legacy price cuts and lower VR lens shipments. We still keep 7% below VA on
4Q26E revenue (NT$7.34bn, −6% QoQ) reflecting muted iPhone sales and no content

in the iPhone 18 Pro's new variable-aperture lens.
CPO component strategy offers a different route to market from Largan
Unlike Largan's pursuit of complete FAU assembly, Genius is targeting high precision
optical components within the module, including: 1) high-precision prism to collimate
light from the PIC into the fibers, 2) V-groove/key slot to precisely hold the fibers in place,
and 3) MT ferrules (high density plastic connector bundling and aligning fibers). We

believe Genius' customer is one of the key module suppliers on NVIDIA's 2H27 program
targeting over 50% share. Our base case models a complete-FAU market of US$1.85bn
in 2028E and US$4.45bn in 2030E, with Genius base case for US$185m and US$668m
representing 10% and 15% revenue share of the complete-FAU revenue TAM.
Valuation: Maintain Buy with  PT lifted to NT$1,250
We fine-tune our 2026/2027 basic EPS estimates from NT$37.2/NT$41.5 to NT$36.7/NT

$44.0 and introduce 2028E EPS of NT$49.25. We move from P/B to P/E SOTP as the core
business now delivers steadier earnings and CPO warrants separate valuation. Our NT
$1,250 PT comprises NT$745 base business  (15x 2028E EPS of NT$49.25) and NT$505
for CPO (40x base case NT$12.72 EPS in 2028E). Catalysts include qualifications at
leading FAU makers and the board approved 7mn share private private placement with
potential strategic investor (albeit 6% dilution if issued). Our US$1.8bn CPO value

compares to US$7bn implied in our Largan NT$5,000 PT.

### Page 2

Genius Electronic Optical UBS Research
Thesis Map     UBS                    Research     THESIS MAP a guide to our thinking and what´s where in this report
Q: Could Genius' CPO products contribute meaningful revenue in a few years?
Yes, from H227E into 2028E. We see Genius' CPO strategy as feasible and lower-risk than Largan's.
Largan's goal is to supply the complete FAU; Genius is entering at the component level, supplying V-
grooves, MT ferrules and prisms to FAU makers. This lets Genius sell into multiple FAU designs and

integrators, limiting its downside if the market converges on a single winning architecture.
Management believes multi-sourcing is inevitable once volumes scale and has seen early indications
of potentially substantial demand from US optical players, including Corning and Coherent. Genius
has a partnership with FOCI and is pursuing other FAU integrators including we believe Coherent;
management suggested further updates could come in the near term. Some parts already ship in

small volumes, but we do not expect meaningful revenue until H227 or 2028.
Q: Will Genius benefit from further lens technology upgrades in the coming years?
Yes. We expect premium lens participation and product mix to support core revenue growth of 8.0%,
9.7% and 9.1% in 2026-28E. We believe Genius is gaining share from Largan in Apple's periscope
lens supply chain after initially lagging. Apple (70–80% of Genius' sales) sell-through units grew 9%

YoY YTD through August according to Counterpoint, and Genius' 9M26 revenue grew 10% YoY on
a similar trajectory. Largan grew only 2% in 9M26, with Q326 down 11% YoY. For the iPhone 18
series, Genius participates in every lens spec except the variable-aperture upgrade, where it stepped
back because of pricing pressure from Chinese competitors. Management says Genius has the
technical capability and could enter if future specs or design changes improve the opportunity.

Genius offers a combination of an established earnings base from smartphone lens and potential
exposure to the passive components required for optical interconnects. We remain constructive on
the lens business and see CPO as a potentially material addition from 2028. The component strategy
provides several routes to customers, but the valuation should reflect uncertainty over qualified
content, allocation and margins. Our Buy rating and NT$1,250 PT is based  on NT$745 base business

(15x 2028E EPS of NT$49.25) plus NT$505 for the CPO earnings (40x base case NT$12.72 EPS in
2028E). The CPO option for Genius is US$1.8bn vs. US$7bn implied in our Largan NT$5,000 PT.
Genius share gains are showing in reported sales, with YTD sales +10% YoY vs. +2% YoY for Largan.
The company is also participating in every spec except variable aperture.  For CPO, Genius delivered
samples of its prism, V-groove, and MT ferrule to FOCI at the end of May, with initial feedback broadly

on track and no major issues reported to date. Our checks indicate the company is also positioning to
supply one of the leading FAU module suppliers for NVIDIA's CPO program from H227.
At NT$1,060, the shares trade at 24.1x our 2027E core EPS and 21.5x our 2028E core EPS, factoring
some of the CPO opportunity in the multiple rather than in estimates relative to the 10 year average
17x P/E. Applying a 15x valuation to the smartphone lens business + 40x to the CPO option from our

scenario of NT$9.30 EPS in 2028E yields upside from the last close at NT$1,060 to a NT$1,250 share
price.

### Page 4

Genius position in the CPO value chain
Genius is targeting the CPO market as a component supplier while Largan is advancing
from components into full-FAU assembly. Genius is sampling three components for CPO
with FAU makers, including: 1) high-precision prism to redirect light from the PIC into
the fibers, 2) V-groove/key slot to precisely hold the fibers in place, and 3) MT ferrules
(high density plastic connector bundling and aligning fibers). The companies overlap in

capability, with Largan also having PMLA and fiber arrays but not getting into some
components like MT ferrules and V-Grooves.
Genius's optical design, precision tooling, metrology and volume-manufacturing
experience offer it a good entry point for this market. Commercial differentiation will
depend on reproducible optical performance, dimensional tolerances, thermal stability
and customer yield.  It has announced collaboration with FOCI and  we believe has

potential to also work with one of NVIDIA's leading module suppliers for its H227
program.

Annual FAU TAM reaches US$1.85bn in 2028E and US$4.5bn in 2030E in

our base case
We update our FAU (fiber array unit) TAM factoring CPO/NPO penetration into
AI networking switches, NVIDIA multi-rack systems, scale-up on compute and switch
boards, and non-NVIDIA accelerators.  We assume one passive FAU assembly connecting
a fiber array to a photonic engine with one unit per optical engine.
We assume one FAU per optical engine and 32 per switch ASIC in our base case,
matching NVIDIA's 32x3.2T engines on its 102.4T Spectrum-X package. Broadcom's

102.4Tb/s Davisson uses 16 engines at 6.4Tb/s, so our bear case assumes an even mix of
these two designs (24 per ASIC). We note that higher switch bandwidth does not
automatically increase FAU count if engine bandwidth rises at the same rate. Our 2030E
assumption maintains the 32 FAUs per switch based on bandwidth rising to 204.8Tb/s

Our base case for the FAU market sums up 4 opportunities for CPO/NPO attach:
1. Scale-out and scale-across switches
We estimate annual switch ASIC shipments x CPO + NPO penetration x optical engines
per ASIC x FAUs per engine. In 2028E, 25% CPO/NPO penetration or 1.0m 102.4T
switch ASICs at 32 FAUs each produces 8.0m FAUs. In 2030E, 55% of 1.1mn

204.8T switch ASICs at 32 FAUs drives a market size of 19.4mn FAUs.

2. NVIDIA's multi-rack connections for Rubin Ultra and Feynman
NVIDIA at its 2026 GTC San Jose proposed a multi-rack option to network together 8
Oberon NVL72 racks for the Rubin Ultra generation and 8 Kyber NVL144 racks.

Feedback at the time showed  possible transceiver or NPO connections for Rubin Ultra
and CPO connections for Feynman.  Our 2028 base case uses 110,000 racks, 15% multi-
rack adoption and 75% CPO/NPO penetration, giving approximately 12,400 connected
racks.
For 2030E, our rack count falls to 35,000 racks as we assume Kyber would have 4x the
number of GPUs, equivalent to 140k Oberon equivalents. We assume the 35,000 racks
at 55% multi-rack adoption x 80% CPO/NPO attach gives 15,400 connected racks. We

assume about 216 and 576 inter-rack FAUs per rack, respectively, driving a market
opportunity of 2.7mn FAUs in 2028E and 8.9mn in 2030E.

Overall market opportunity: 12.3mn/35.6mn generates a US$1.85bn/US
$4.5bn TAM in 2028/2030E
We sum four pools: scale-out/scale-across switches, NVIDIA multi-rack pods, CPO native
NVIDIA GPUs and non-NVIDIA scale-up.  We add 10%/5% for FAUs lost at downstream
attach and test and for field spares, driving a market size of in our base case of 12.3mn
FAUs in 2028E and 35.6mn in 2030E. At US$150/US$125 per FAU, that is a US$1.85bn

market in 2028E and US$4.5bn in 2030E (bear/bull US$0.5bn/US$3.5bn in 2028E; US
$1.4bn/US$9.0bn in 2030E). The expansion to 2030E comes from wider optical
adoption and greater content in scale-up systems, despite an assumed FAU ASP decline
from US$150 to US$125.

Scenario analysis on Genius CPO opportunity

### Page 7

We assume in base case Genius captures 10% of the TAM in 2028E and 15% by 2030E.
The base case in 2028E is consistent with US$30 of prism, V-groove and ferrule content
in half of all FAUs or US$15 average content spread across the FAU market.  The
2030E bull case (25%) or US$37.50 would require near full content of some of the sub-
components or otherwise ability to integrate more of the solution value by that time.
Our bear case assumes only 1% share of the opportunity in 2028E and 5% of the

opportunity in 2030E, with EPS even by 2030E falling to NT$4 and yielding limited
valuation potential for the shares.

Source: UBS estimates
Reiterate Buy and raise PT to NT$1,250 valuing in CPO optionality

We adjust our valuation method from P/B to P/E SOTP to better reflect the company’s
core business and its opportunity within CPO industry while the core smartphone
business is demonstrating more stable earnings to use P/E vs. prior P/B. Our PT at NT
$1,250 is based on NT$745 base business  (15x 2028E EPS of NT$49.25) plus NT$505
for the CPO earnings (40x base case NT$12.72 EPS in 2028E) with catalysts from
upcoming qualifications into leading FAU makers and potential strategic private

placement with board approving 7mn share private placement (albeit 6% dilution if
issued). We note Genius CPO option valued at US$1.8bn vs. US$7bn implied in our
Largan valuation.
Our price target uses 2028 earnings for both businesses, reflecting the modeled start of
meaningful CPO contribution. We apply 15x core EPS of NT$49.25 and 40x CPO EPS of
NT$12.72 for a NT$1,250 price target. The 40x multiple assumes the optical business
can sustain strong growth after its initial ramp; qualification, concentration and margin

risk limit the confidence that can be placed on that earnings stream.
In the upside case, stronger premium lens participation and manufacturing execution lift
2028E core EPS to NT$54.32. Faster optical adoption expands the 2028E FAU market to
US$3.46bn; 15% effective revenue capture and a 31% pretax margin produce
approximately NT$36.88 CPO EPS. Applying 15x to core earnings and 42x to CPO
earnings gives approximately NT$2,365.
In the downside case, weaker lens demand and margin pressure lower core EPS to NT

$44.29. Slower optical adoption leaves a US$0.46bn FAU market, of which Genius
captures 1%, producing only NT$0.29 CPO EPS. Applying 12x and 38x gives
approximately NT$540. The downside is driven by both a weaker core business and a
much smaller optical contribution.

Genius Electronic Optical UBS Research
Upside/Downside Spectrum
Upside/Downside Spectrum
Genius (3406.TW) trading at 1,060 at closed Oct 8.
Source: UBS estimates
UPSIDE (NT$2,365): Lens upgrades accelerate and Genius gains share in higher-end
specs. 2027E/28E sales grow 11.8%/9.1% YoY (base 9.7%/9.1%), and GM expands to
35.6%/35.5% (base 34.4%/34.3%) on better yields and mix. 2028E core EPS of NT
$54.3 (+10% vs base) at 15x gives NT$815. Faster CPO adoption and stronger AI capex

lift the 2028E FAU TAM to US$3.5bn (19.8mn units at US$175); at 15% capture, CPO
sales reach US$519mn and CPO EPS NT$36.9, worth NT$1,550 at 42x.
BASE (NT$1,250):  Core NT$745 (15x NT$49.25) plus CPO NT$505 (40x NT$12.72), as
above.
DOWNSIDE (NT540): Smartphone demand weakens, legacy price erosion continues
and share gains stall. 2027E sales grow 7.4% YoY with GM of 33.4%, and 2028E core
EPS falls to NT$44.3 (−10% vs base), worth NT$530 at 12x. CPO adoption is slow

(10%/3% CPO/NPO in scale-out, no ASIC attach), leaving a US$0.46bn TAM; at 1%
capture, CPO is worth NT$11.

Company Description
Founded in 1989, Genius Electronic Optical (GSEO) is a leading global manufacturer of
camera lenses, providing full service optical solutions, including mobile lenses, automotive
lenses and AR/VR optics. Its production sites are in Taichung (one plant) and Xiamen (two

plants). The Taichung plant mainly manufactures VR lenses, while the Xiamen plants produce
compact lenses for consumer electronics. Optical lens products accounted for 73% of 2023
total revenue, and others include TOF lenses, flash lenses, VR lenses and LED lighting
products. Its major client is Apple and it supplies lenses for iPhones and iPads.
UBS EPS and Consensus EPS
The UBS EPS is an adjusted diluted EPS metric. It is calculated using the UBS analyst's

interpretation of earnings suitable for valuation purposes divided by the diluted number of
shares. This may differ to the way the consensus EPS metric has been calculated.
Valuation Method and Risk Statement
Our Genius price target is based on PE-based sum-of-the-parts model. Genius' growth is
highly leveraged to the overall handset industry and could be affected by volatility in handset
demand, as well as whether handset OEMs will continue upgrading their camera design.

Genius' margins could be affected by increasing competition and the rise of new
technologies, ASP pressure from customers and fluctuating material and labour costs.
Downside risks include competitors catching up in technology, yield rate issues for new
products and uncertain iPhone demand.
Our price target is based on SOTP valuation on the CPO opportunity and traditional lens
business. We think Largan's downside risks include: 1) ongoing pricing pressure from

smartphone OEM clients and increasingly intense competition among lens suppliers; and 2) a
slower-thanexpected smartphone demand recovery in China, and 3) CPO commercial success
below the market's elevated expectations. We believe the key upside risk is a faster-than-
expected CPO qualification process at Largan. In addition, if the company demonstrates
exceptionally efficient and highly accurate automation capabilities, it could support greater
market share gains within the industry than we currently anticipate.

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

耕興 (6146 TT)  Buy

耕興評價已修正，有機會受惠轉單效應，給 Buy 的投資建
議。  
投資建議 ：美國 FCC 有機率禁止，銷往美國的電子產品在中國
實驗室進行測試，耕興有望接受大量轉單，給 Buy 的投資建議。  
公司簡介： 耕興主要從事電子產品之電磁相容 (EMC) 認證、安規
檢測服務、無線網通認證、手機認證、數位電視認證及防磁零
組件之銷售，全球知名的手機、平板電腦與 NB 業者、晶片廠、
網通廠等多為耕興的客戶。  
耕興的 5G 產品、高階網通的檢測市佔率全球第一： FCC 公布
1H26 的 5G 產品、高階網通檢測的耕興市佔率，分別為 23.97% 、
47.3% ，皆為全球第一。  
FCC 將投票決定中國實驗室未來的商機 ： 路透社 (Reuters) 報導 ，
FCC 將於 10/29/2026 投票表決，來決定賣到美國的電子產品，
是否要禁止在中國實驗室 (非 MRA 國家 )進行測試 ， 若表決通過 ，
預計將於 12/2028 開始生效。  
若 FCC 表決通過，對耕興有利： 耕興具備 FCC 的 MRA 資格，
若 FCC 禁止中國實驗室 ， 將會有大量相關訂單轉移至台灣廠商 ，
耕興檢測產能為全球第一，將直接受惠轉單效應。

2 群益投顧 
  ESG ：  
Environmental ：與 2021 年相比， 2022 年之用電度數較 2021 年減少 0.68% 、
減紙 42%( 減碳 7.92 公噸 CO 2 )。未來規劃：華亞實驗室建置太陽能發電系
統；建置雨水或空調冷凝水回收系統；分階段換發 LED 節能燈具、省電空
調；並於馬達設備增設變頻器以節省用電。  
Social ：體恤員工與資助社團法人嘉義縣扶緣服務協會、新港文教基金會、
與新港奉天宮之金虎爺慢跑活動。國內交易量一百萬元以上的供應商中，
92% 已簽屬「供應商社會責任承諾書」並定期查核；零件部跟生產相關的原
料供應商皆已簽署「不使用衝突礦產聲明書」。  
Governance ：董事會治理制度包含「董事會議事規範」、衡量績效目標落實
之「董事績效評估辦法」、確保誠信經營之「誠信經營守則」、避免貪腐與
符合公平交易法之「誠信經營作業程序及行為指南」等。訂定「資訊安全政
策規範」並以 PDCA (Plan, Do, Check, Act) 循環方式管理。  
公司簡介：  
耕興成立於 1997 年，主要從事電子產品之電磁相容 (EMC) 認證、安規檢測
服務、無線網通認證、手機認證、數位電視認證及防磁零組件之銷售，全球
知名的手機、平板電腦與 NB 業者、晶片廠、網通廠等多為耕興的客戶。  
由於耕興具備 GCF 、 PTCRB 組織的符合性檢測認證，以及加拿大與日本發
證權，只要客戶通過公司認證後即可銷往多國，可降低檢驗所需時間與成
本。  
根據 2022 年 FCC 所公布的資料，耕興在 5G/Wi-Fi 的檢測市占率已經是世
界第一，產能也是全球第一。手機 /網通賣的款式越多，對耕興越有利，單
一產品的出貨量對耕興營收無關。  
台灣無線通訊檢測相關同業包含東研信超 (6840 TT) 、穩得 (6761 TT) 和敦吉
(2459 TT) ，耕興的策略是不斷地去投入比同業多的 CAPEX ，做最先進規格
的檢測，像是毫米波、 Wi-Fi 7 的檢測，以提升營收與獲利。  
營收占比 _地區別：  
浩興為耕興的中國子公司。

資料來源：耕興法說簡報。

### Page 3

個股報告  
                                        2026 年 10 月 14 日

3 群益投顧 
AI 筆電、企業級 AP 檢測需求帶動耕興 1H26 營運成長：  
耕興表示， 1H26 獲利增長主要來自於實體 AI 終端裝置發展同時結合非
MRA(Mutual Recognition Arrangement ，相互承認協議 ) 國家地區 FCC 
PAG(Pre-Approval Guidance) 轉單效應帶動大量高階機種檢測需求。  
耕興內部統計最顯著增長的兩大產業族群來自於 AI 筆電及北美企業級 AP
檢測訂單需求增加。  
2H26 營運有望逐季成長：  
展望 2H26 同樣伴隨 PAG 轉單效應持續消化大量湧入的 AI 筆電及北美企業
級 AP 檢測訂單。檢測需求帶動下， 3Q26 獲利表現將有機會比 2Q26 強勁，
同時 4Q26 的獲利也有機會更上一層樓， 4Q26 是 2027 年高階旗艦級手機種
換機潮的檢測高峰期。  
耕興的 5G 產品、高階網通的檢測市佔率全球第一：  
FCC 公布 1H26 的 5G 產品、高階網通檢測的耕興市佔率，分別為 23.97% 、
47.3% ，皆為全球第一。  
FCC 將投票決定中國實驗室未來的商機：  
路透社 (Reuters) 報導， FCC 將於 10/29/2026 投票表決，來決定賣到美國的電
子產品，是否要禁止在中國實驗室 (非 MRA 國家 )進行測試，若表決通過，
預計將於 12/2028 開始生效。  
FCC 主席表示， 2025 年賣到美國的電子產品，約有 82% 是在中國實驗室進
行測試，而在美國實驗室測試的比例不到 4%。  
若 FCC 表決通過，對耕興有利：  
耕興具備 FCC 的 MRA 資格，若 FCC 禁止中國實驗室，將會有大量相關訂
單轉移至台灣廠商，耕興檢測產能為全球第一，將直接受惠轉單效應。

7 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】  
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間 ≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy) 5% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等  
預期近期股價將處於盤整  
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

宏观研究|宏观跟踪报告

[Table_Title] 
财政支出有望继续提速

[Table_ReportType] 
——9 月财政数据跟踪

[Table_ReportDate] 2026 年 10 月 26 日

[T able_Summary] 
⚫ 事件：1-9 月，全国一般性公共预算收入当月同比增速-
0.12%，上月值为4.68%。全国一般性公共预算支出当月同
比增速0.03%，前值为-0.13%。 
⚫ 财政收入当月同比转为负增，土地相关税种对地方财政拖
累持续。9 月，税收、非税收入增速均持续放缓。中央财
政收入下滑速度加快，地方财政收入持续回落。契税跌幅
扩大，房产税边际上行。地方政府土地出让收入累计同比
为-27.9%，土地增值税增速由正转负，耕地占用税跌幅加
深，城镇土地使用税增速也明显下滑。 
⚫ 国内增值税为主要拉动，消费税跌幅收窄，出口退税大幅
增长给税收收入带来拖累。企业所得税同比回落，原材料
价格上涨给企业盈利带来一定压力，个人所得税同比下行
斜率放缓。工业和服务业生产改善，工业品价格上行，国
内增值税上行。必选消费边际修复，9 月1 日起对部分电
池开征消费税政策落地，国内消费税同比跌幅收窄。出口
保持强劲，关税增速上行，外贸出口退税增速由-1.7%大
幅拉升至29%。9 月股票市场震荡回落，成交额缩量，叠
加去年同期高基数（+342.4%）影响，证券交易印花税增
速转负。 
⚫ 财政支出提速，但进程依然偏缓。财政支出当月同比增速
转正，但累计同比持续回落。从投放进程来看，9 月边际
提速，整体进展仍低于近几年均值。中央财政支出高位小
幅放缓，仍为主要支撑；地方支出小幅修复上行，但地方
化债和土地收入约束下，地方财政依然承压。 
⚫ 民生类支出回落，基建内部分化，科技支撑高位边际放
缓。民生方面，社会保障和就业、卫生健康回落幅度较
大，文旅传媒、教育增速上行。民生类支出拨付节奏前
置，单月增速放缓。科学技术增速高位回落，但仍为政策
重点方向。基建方面，交通运输、农林水事务增速下跌，
而城乡社区事务同比增速大幅上行，地方化债和项目储备
不足，传统基建改善不均衡。债务付息增速转负，部分受
付息节奏错位和基数抬升影响，对文旅传媒等非刚性财政
支出的压减节奏有所放缓。但当前债务负担依然较大，付
息压力仍难缓解。 
⚫ 政府性基金收入小幅修复上行，土地财政退坡约束下，地
方财政支出依然受限。地产销售“金九”行情表现疲弱，
房企资金来源受限，土地市场景气度持续偏低。土地财政

退坡仍是地方财政支出的主要约束。全国政府性基金支出

工具落地进程

产投资回落幅度超预期。

### Page 4

[Table_Pagehead]  
证券研究报告|宏观报告

万联证券研究所 www.wlzq.cn 第 4 页 共 8 页  
事件 
2026年1-9月，全国一般性公共预算收入累计同比由5.7%回落至5.1%，当月同比增速
-0.12%，上月值为4.68%。全国一般性公共预算支出累计同比由1.2%回落至1%，当月
同比增速0.03%，前值为-0.13%。

1    财政收入持续回落，出口退税拖累明显 
财政收入当月同比转为负增，完成预算目标进程依然较快。1-9月，全国一般公共预
算收入累计同比由5.7%回落至5.1%，当月同比增速-0.12%，年内首次出现负增，前值
为4.68%。从收入节奏来看，1-9月累计完成全年预算收入目标的78.07%， 仍处于近五
年同期的高点。居民信贷需求放缓， 加杠杆意愿不足， 内需依然偏弱。企业生产保持
旺盛，但成本端压力抬升和内需偏弱的影响下，企业投资扩产相对谨慎。 
中央财政收入下滑速度加快， 地方财政收入持续回落， 土地相关税种对地方财政拖累
持续。9月， 中央财政收入同比增速由9.27%回落至0.18%， 地方财政收入由0.71%下行
至-0.34%， 地方财政收入依然承压。 房产税当月同比由0%上行至2.92%,契税跌幅扩大。
地方政府土地出让收入9月累计同比由-28.6%小幅收窄为-27.9%，土地增值税增速由
正转负，耕地占用税跌幅加深，城镇土地使用税增速也明显下滑。

图表1：财政收入当月同比增速转负（%） 图表2：公共财政收入各月进度（%）

资料来源：Wind，万联证券研究所 资料来源：Wind，万联证券研究所

非税、税收收入增速均持续放缓。1-9月， 税收收入累计同比增速由6.6%回落至6.1%，
9月当月同比增速由5.6%下滑至0.2%。 非税收入累计同比增速由1.5%小幅回落至1.2%，
当月同比增速由0.3%下滑至-1%。 
各税种表现分化，国内增值税为主要拉动， 消费税跌幅收窄， 外需强劲拉动相关税种
表现，出口退税大幅增长给税收收入带来拖累。分税种来看，9月，企业所得税同比
增速由11.6%回落至-0.4%， 原材料价格上涨给企业盈利带来一定压力， 个人所得税同
比由11.8%回落至10.5%，下行斜率放缓。工业和服务业生产改善，工业品价格上行，
国内增值税由4%上涨至7.3%。必选消费边际修复，9月1日起对部分电池开征消费税政
策落地，国内消费税同比跌幅由-14%收窄至-8.5%。出口保持强劲，外贸相关税种表

2    财政支出增速转正，地方财力偏紧制约支出扩张 
财政支出当月同比增速转正，投放提速， 但支出进程依然偏缓， 地方财政支出小幅修
复。2026年9月，全国一般性公共预算支出累计同比由1.2%回落至1%，当月同比增速
0.03%，前值为-0.13%。从投放进程来看，达到全年预算的70.04%，低于近几年同期
水平。9月投放进程边际提速，但支出整体进展依然偏慢。四季度财政支出仍有较大
发力空间。中央财政支出当月同比增速由4.7%下行至4.6%,地方财政支出由-1.1%收窄
至-0.8%,地方财政支出增速上行， 但地方化债和土地收入约束下， 地方财政依然承压。

政府性基金收入小幅修复上行，土地财政退坡约束下，地方财政支出依然受限。1-9
月，全国政府性基金收入累计同比增速由-19%继续上升至-18.6%， 地方本级政府性基
金收入累计同比增速由 -22.9%回升至-22.3%。地方土地出让收入累计同比增速由 -
28.6%收窄至-27.9%，地产销售“金九”行情表现疲弱，房企资金来源受限，土地市
场景气度持续偏低。 土地财政退坡仍是地方财政支出的主要约束。全国政府性基金支
出累计同比增速由-17%回升至-16.2%，随着9月政府债发行提速，增速逐步修复，但
资金到位形成实物工作量仍存在时滞，四季度有望持续修复。 
民生类支出节奏前置， 单月增速放缓， 基建内部分化， 科技高位边际放缓。分支出类
型看，9月，民生方面，社会保障和就业当月同比由2.6%下跌至-4.8%,卫生健康也回
落幅度较大，文旅传媒增速由-9.6%上行至1.6%，教育上行2.2个百分点至3.5%,节能
环保同比修复上行， 但增速仍处低位。 民生类支出拨付节奏前置， 单月增速放缓。科
学技术增速放缓， 但仍为政策重点方向。 基建类支出内部分化加剧， 其中，交通运输
跌幅进一步加深， 农林水事务增速由正转负， 城乡社区事务同比增速大幅上行，地方
化债和项目储备不足，传统基建改善不均衡。 
债务付息增速转负，但并非趋势性拐点。9月债务付息当月同比增速由7%降至-1.2%,
部分受付息节奏错位和基数抬升影响 ，对文旅传媒等非刚性财政支出的压减节奏有
所放缓。但当前债务负担依然较大，付息压力仍难缓解。

图表7：城乡社区事务、文旅体媒同比大幅修复（%） 图表8：政府性基金收支及土地出让收入走势（%）

3    财政赤字季节性走扩 
财政赤字季节性走扩。1-9月，财政盈余由上月的24,818亿元上行至37,907亿元，赤
字水平较去年同期已经有所回落。 
四季度财政支持力度加大， 支出有望迎来提速。财政支出进程仍有待进一步提速，财
政资金向基建支出边际倾斜，新型政策性金融工具和地方政府债务结存限额将给四
季度基建带来资金支持， “六张网”建设将为重点方向。财政土地出让收入缩减的拖
累仍将持续，四季度仍需关注政策工具落地进程

### Page 1

www.imeritz.com

삼성바이오로직스 (207940)

Company Brief 
제약/바이오 
Analyst 김준영 
junyoung.kim@meritz.co.kr 
RA 김현비 
hyunbee.kim@meritz.co.kr 외형 성장 지속, 수익성 개선은 시간이 필요

3Q26 Review  
3Q26 연결 기준 매출액 1조 4,903억원(YoY +18.5%, QoQ +12.8%), 영업이익 
6,401억원(YoY +1.0%, QoQ +9.2%)으로 컨센서스를 각각 9.8%, 4.7% 상회했다. 
기존 1 ~ 4공장 풀가동 및 미국 Rockville 공장 매출 약 1,000억원과 5공장 PPQ 
물량 인식이 더해지며 외형 성장을 견인했다. 반면 신규 생산시설의 초기 비용 및 제
품 믹스 영향으로 OPM은 42.9%를 기록하며 전년 동기 대비 7.5%p 하락했다. 동
사는 연간 매출 성장률 가이던스를 기존 +15~20%에서 +20%로 상향했다. 당사는 
4분기 Rockville 공장 매출 기여와 기존 공장의 높은 가동률을 감안하여 연간 매출
액 5조 6,135억원(YoY +23.2%)을 예상한다.

신규 생산거점의 외형 확대, 이익 기여는 좀 더 긴 호흡으로 
5공장은 2Q26부터 PPQ 생산을 본격화했으나 상업 생산에 따른 실질적인 이익 기
여는 2027년 하반기에 확대될 전망이다. Rockville 공장은 3Q와 4Q 각각 약 
1,000억원의 매출이 예상되며 2027년 분기별 매출도 유사한 수준을 유지할 것으로 
전망한다. 다만 영업이익 기여까지는 시간이 더 소요될 것으로 예상되어 2027년 이
익 성장은 미국 공장보다 5공장 Ramp-up이 주도할 전망이다.

투자의견 Buy, 적정주가 200만원 유지 
삼성바이오로직스에 대해 투자의견 Buy, 적정주가 200만원을 유지한다. 고객사 수주 
문의가 회복되는 가운데 대형 계약 협의가 진행 중이며 6공장 역시 연말 또는 내년 
초 착공을 목표로 하고 있다. 다만 신규 수주가 실제 계약으로 이어지는 시점과 5공
장의 상업 생산 전환 속도가 중기 실적의 핵심 변수로 판단한다. 추가 대형 수주 확
보 시 5공장 이후의 성장 가시성도 높아질 것으로 예상한다. 
PolyPeptide 인수는 펩타이드 CDMO로 사업을 확장한다는 점에서 긍정적이다. 인
수 완료 이후 다음 분기부터 연결 매출 인식이 가능하나 유의미한 영업이익 기여까지
는 최소 2년 이상 소요될 전망이다. PolyPeptide 인수는 현재 추정치에서 반영하지 
않았으며 향후 인수 완료 시 연결 OPM 희석 가능성을 감안할 필요가 있다. 한편 최
근 법원 항고심에서 배양부터 정제까지의 생산공정이 쟁의행위 기간에도 정상 수행되
어야 할 보전작업으로 인정되면서 파업에 따른 생산 차질 우려는 완화될 것으로 판단
한다. 노사 협상은 여전히 진행 중이나 생산 안정성에 대한 고객사 우려가 낮아질 경
우 신규 수주에도 긍정적으로 작용할 전망이다. 
 Meritz Research  2026. 10. 22

Buy (20거래일 평균종가 대비 상승 여력 기준)

### Page 1

甲⻣⽂宣布引⼊ Cerebras AI 芯⽚，预计将于 2027 年上半年正式部署；除 Cerebras 外，甲
⻣⽂还宣布了⼀系列芯⽚供应商合作协议，涵盖英伟达、⾕歌及英特尔⽀持的 SambaNova
Systems 。在与英伟达的合作上，甲⻣⽂表⽰将把部署规模扩展⾄ " 多吉⽡级别 " ，采⽤英伟
达最新⼀代 "Vera Rubin"AI 服务器。
摘要：
甲⻣⽂宣布将在其云服务中引⼊ Cerebras Systems 的 AI 芯⽚，此举标志着这家英伟达最具挑战
性的新兴竞争对⼿迎来⼜⼀重量级客⼾， AI 推理芯⽚市场的竞争格局正在加速演变。
甲⻣⽂联席⾸席执⾏官 Clay Magouyrk 周⼆在公司年度客⼾⼤会上宣布了这⼀合作，预计
Cerebras 系统将于 2027 年上半年正式部署于 Oracle Cloud Infrastructure 。
与此同时，甲⻣⽂在⼤会上密集宣布了⼀系列芯⽚供应商合作协议，除 Cerebras 外，还涵盖英
伟达、⾕歌及英特尔⽀持的 SambaNova Systems 。

消息公布后， Cerebras 股价盘中⼀度涨超 10% 。不过，⾃该公司今年 5 ⽉上市以来，截⾄本周⼀
收盘，其股价累计跌幅仍⾼达 41% 。
甲⻣⽂⼤举扩充芯⽚版图
此次合作是甲⻣⽂⼤规模数据中⼼扩张战略的最新动作。甲⻣⽂在⼤会上密集宣布了⼀系列芯
⽚ 供 应 商 合 作 协 议 ， 除 Cerebras 外 ， 还 涵 盖 英 伟 达 、 ⾕ 歌 及 英 特 尔 ⽀ 持 的 SambaNova
Systems 。
在与英伟达的合作上，甲⻣⽂表⽰将把部署规模扩展⾄ " 多吉⽡级别 " ，采⽤英伟达最新⼀
代 "Vera Rubin"AI 服务器。
英伟达 CEO ⻩仁勋此前曾表⽰， Vera Rubin 系统每吉⽡可为英伟达带来最⾼ 400 亿美元的营收，
但双⽅均未披露此次协议的具体⾦额。
值得注意的是，即便在部署英伟达硬件的系统中，甲⻣⽂也选择以⾃研的 "Acceleron" ⽹络芯⽚
替代英伟达的部分⽹络产品，进⼀步强化了其在基础设施层⾯的⾃主化布局。

此外，甲⻣⽂还宣布⾸次引⼊⾕歌的 TPU （张量处理器）芯⽚。
甲⻣⽂云基础设施总裁 Mahesh Thiagarajan 表⽰： " 不同 AI ⼯作负载具有不同的计算需求，多元
化的选择赋予客⼾灵活性，使其能够根据每项⼯作负载的性能、吞吐量和经济效益选择最适合
的基础设施。 "
Cerebras ：规模扩张仍难释投资者疑虑
对于 Cerebras ⽽⾔，甲⻣⽂的⼊局意义重⼤。 Cerebras 的核⼼技术在于采⽤占据整块硅晶圆的
超⼤芯⽚，⽽⾮将晶圆切割为多个处理器的传统⽅式，公司主张这⼀架构能⼤幅降低 AI 推理请求
的响应延迟。
" 这是我们⼀级客⼾群体的重⼤扩展， "Cerebras CEO Andrew Feldman 表⽰， " 我们正在以⼤
规模⽅式被部署于其他公司的数据中⼼。 "

该 公 司 此 前 已 签 下 OpenAI 及 亚 ⻢ 逊 云 服 务 等 知 名 客 ⼾ 。 据 彭 博 汇 总 的 分 析 师 平 均 预 测 ，
Cerebras 明年营收有望达到约 30 亿美元，较今年实现约三倍增⻓，⽽今年全年营收预计仍不⾜
10 亿美元。
尽管如此，投资者的疑虑并未消散。英伟达当前每季度 AI 芯⽚销售额接近 1000 亿美元，在市场
规模上对 Cerebras 形成压倒性优势。
在英伟达主导训练芯⽚市场的背景下， Cerebras 与 SambaNova 均将突破⼝锁定于竞争更为分散
的推理市场，但能否在此站稳脚跟，仍是投资者持续关注的核⼼问题。
资本⽀出计划与融资安排待厘清
在资本⽀出层⾯，甲⻣⽂尚未披露计划采购的 Cerebras 产品规模及芯⽚购买的具体融资⽅式。
上⽉财报电话会议上，甲⻣⽂表⽰客⼾正越来越多地⾃⾏承担芯⽚采购成本，并维持 2027 财年
约 900 亿⾄ 950 亿美元的资本⽀出⽬标。
此前甲⻣⽂曾表⽰，将在截⾄ 2027 年 5 ⽉的财年内投⼊ 700 亿美元资本开⽀，以图跻⾝顶级 AI 算

⼒供应商⾏列。
甲⻣⽂本周还计划举⾏投资者⽇活动，届时或将就数据中⼼扩张策略及资本配置安排披露更多
细节。

#cliffnotes #rba #australia #inflation #us #fomc #ecb #aud #usd
Cliff Notes: mulling the minutes
By Sian Fenner, Ryan Wells & Illiana Jain
11:10 October 09 2026 Share
Key insights from the week that was.
The only data release of note in Australia this week was the October Westpac-MI Consumer Sentiment
Survey, which reported a 4.7% drop in the headline index. At 80.4, sentiment has tumbled back to
deeply pessimistic levels, similar to the post-pandemic cost-of-living shock when readings were among

the weakest in the survey’s more than fifty-year history. Indeed, inflation and interest rate pressures are
once again clearly front of mind for consumers, with both the view on ‘family finances vs a year ago’

### Page 2

and ‘family finance next 12 months’ falling 8.0% and 6.4% in the month, respectively. Moreover, the
RBA’s September rate hike looks to have had a significant impact – sentiment tanked 20% between
those surveyed before the RBA decision and those surveyed afterwards.

Views on the economic outlook were relatively more resilient, but they still weakened, with the one-year
and five-year ahead sub-indexes falling 1.6% and 0.4% respectively. Consumers are becoming more
‘on edge’ about job security, albeit not outright alarmed. The unemployment rate remains relatively low
versus history, although it has drifted up to a cycle high of 4.6% as labour supply continues to outstrip
demand. We expect it to hold there in next week’s September Labour Force Survey – see our preview
for more detail. All told, with higher interest rates, renewed inflation pressures and a softening jobs
market all weighing at once, households are entering the final months of the year on the back foot.

Offshore, the focus was on US data.

Before turning to this week's releases, it is worth noting that September non-farm payrolls came in
softer than expected across most metrics. Employment rose by only 29k (89k expected), and there
were sizeable downward revisions to prior months, with a combined 60k removed from July and August
payrolls, leaving gains of just 31k and 29k respectively. The details pointed to weaker hiring in leisure &
hospitality and education & health services industries, although construction and manufacturing
employment continued to benefit from ongoing data-centre-related investment. The unemployment rate
edged up to 4.2%, with softer employment occurring alongside a rise in labour force participation to
61.8%. Meanwhile wage growth moderated to 0.1%mth (3.0%yr). Taken together, the report suggests
labour demand is cooling, with the three-month average pace of job creation slowing to around 50k.
However, softer payroll gains are not necessarily indicative of labour market deterioration. Lower
immigration and ageing demographics have reduced the pace of employment growth needed to keep
the unemployment rate stable. As such, the report is more consistent with a labour market that is
gradually normalising than one entering a meaningful downturn.

The September FOMC minutes reinforced a distinctly hawkish message, with participants noting they
had "not seen sufficient progress on lowering inflation in recent months" and expressing concern that
inflation risks remained "skewed to the upside". Policymakers highlighted that both core services and
core goods inflation remained elevated, with some attributing persistent goods price strength to the
effects of the AI buildout despite the sector's traditional disinflationary role. There was also concern
that, after more than five years of inflation above target, elevated inflation could begin to influence
inflation expectations and wage- and price-setting behaviour. In contrast, labour market conditions
were generally viewed as stable and "close to maximum employment". While a majority noted the
labour market had strengthened somewhat recently, several participants highlighted unusually low
dynamism, characterised by low hiring, low layoffs, a low job-finding rate and elevated long-term
unemployment. Importantly, participants generally judged that aggregate wage growth was moderate
and "consistent with inflation moving toward 2 percent", with several explicitly noting that "the labor
market was not currently a source of inflationary pressures".

### Page 3

Related articles
A key focus was the interaction between AI investment, productive capacity and inflation. While
participants broadly expected AI-related investment to boost productivity growth and potential output
over time, several warned that "the AI buildout could cause aggregate demand to outpace aggregate
supply over the medium term, putting upward pressure on inflation". Consistent with this view, some
participants had raised their estimate of the neutral policy rate and several judged the current policy
stance to be "not restrictive or only mildly restrictive". Against this backdrop, all participants supported
the September rate increase, and most assessed that another increase in the federal funds rate would
likely be appropriate by year-end, although future decisions would remain data dependent.

The September ISM Services PMI released this week painted a similar picture of an economy that
remains resilient but is gradually losing momentum. The headline index eased to 54.9 from 55.4 in
August, with the decline driven by a sharp fall in business activity and a 9.4pt drop in new export
orders, which slipped into contractionary territory for the first time in eight months. Labour market
conditions improved modestly with the employment index moving to broadly neutral territory 50.1 after
two months below 50, supported by elevated backlogs and still-solid demand. Respondent
commentary illustrated the mixed nature of labour demand, with some firms reporting they were "filling
positions vacated due to promotions or retirements", while others cited "restructuring due to efficiencies
gained using AI tools". Cost pressures also remained elevated, with the prices paid index rising to 74.0,
its highest level since July 2022 and around 18pts above its historical average, while fuel costs, tariffs
and supply chain constraints remained the most frequently cited concerns. Overall, the report suggests
services activity remains consistent with ongoing expansion, albeit at a slower pace, while labour
demand and pricing pressures remain firm.

Overall, the data supports our view that the FOMC will move to a broadly neutral policy stance by the
end of the year with one more rate hike, although the case for a back-to-back move in October has
become less clear cut.

F I R S T  T O  M A R K E T

CTCP VÀNG BẠC ĐÁ QUÝ PHÚ NHUẬN (PNJ)  
Cập nhật ĐHĐCĐ bất thường 21/10/2026

Tái cơ cấu để xử lý áp lực thanh khoản  
— ĐHĐCĐ bất thường PNJ ngày 21/10/2026 đã thông qua điều chỉnh kế hoạch kinh 
doanh và phương án phát hành riêng lẻ để bổ sung vốn tái cấu trúc. Ban lãnh đạo đặt 
ra các vấn đề trọng yếu là đáp ứng nhu cầu thanh khoản và chuẩn bị nguồn lực để thực 
hiện các cam kết thu đổi đã tích lũy trong quá khứ. Bên cạnh đó, các cổ đông tham dự 
cũng đặt các câu hỏi liên quan đến tình hình hoạt động qua các điểm chính (1) dòng 
tiền hoạt động kinh doanh; (2) diễn biến thị trường sau khi PNJ áp dụng chính sách thu 
đổi mới; (3) hiệu quả hoạt động sau tái cấu trúc.

Nội dung chính tại đại hội 
— Theo Ban lãnh đạo, thông tin liên quan đến P-Lab từ đầu tháng 7 khiến khách hàng gia 
tăng bán lại sản phẩm. Tổng giá trị thu đổi và mua lại trong tháng đạt gần 6,000 tỷ VND, 
so với khoảng 7,000 tỷ VND trong cả năm 2025. Theo BCTC riêng, nguồn tiền khả dụng 
giảm từ khoảng 3,700 tỷ VND cuối Q2/2026 xuống 770 tỷ VND cuối Q3/2026, đã bao 
gồm khoản vay 700 tỷ VND từ Chủ tịch HĐQT. Tỷ trọng kim cương và trang sức kim 
cương trong tổng tồn kho tăng từ 41% lên 52%. 
— Q3/2026 ghi nhận lỗ sau thuế 258 tỷ VND trước phần tăng trích lập dự phòng. Để giảm 
áp lực thanh toán, PNJ kéo dài thời gian chi trả đối với kim cương lên 120 ngày từ tháng 
07/2026. Thay đổi chính sách cũng ảnh hưởng đến niềm tin khách hàng và sức mua, cụ 
thể doanh thu và lợi nhuận gộp Q3/2026 đạt lần lượt 6,995/609 tỷ VND, giảm 14%/62% 
YoY. Chi phí bán hàng và quản lý giảm 13% YoY chưa đủ bù đắp mức giảm lợi nhuận 
gộp, khiến PNJ lỗ sau thuế 258 tỷ VND, so với lãi 496 tỷ VND trong Q3/2025.

Biểu đồ 1. Kết quả kinh doanh theo quý  Biểu đồ 2. Diễn biến doanh thu thuần/ngày và doanh thu bán 
lẻ/ngày theo quý

F I R S T  T O  M A R K E T 
 — PNJ nâng giả định tỷ lệ thu đổi trên giá trị thu đổi còn lại lên 80%, so với 28.8% đối với 
nhóm sản phẩm giai đoạn 2020–2026 trong ước tính tại BCTC bán niên 2026. Tổng dự 
phòng tổn thất dự kiến tăng từ 2,267 tỷ VND lên 7,071 tỷ VND. Kế hoạch điều chỉnh ghi 
nhận doanh thu 39,057 tỷ VND, lỗ trước thuế 6,059 tỷ VND và lỗ sau thuế 6,271 tỷ VND. 
Theo Ban lãnh đạo, LNST loại trừ dự phòng ước khoảng 800 tỷ VND, chủ yếu nhờ kết 
quả Q1/2026. Khoản dự phòng phản ánh tổn thất ước tính cho toàn bộ kịch bản, không 
đồng nghĩa toàn bộ giá trị sẽ được chi trả ngay trong kỳ. 
— Giá trị cam kết thu đổi tích lũy đối với kim cương và trang sức kim cương tại cuối 
Q3/2026 được PNJ ước tính khoảng 21,000 tỷ VND. Với hàng đã bán, doanh nghiệp dự 
kiến chuyển đổi quyền lợi khách hàng sang sản phẩm và các chương trình ưu đãi. Với 
sản phẩm bán mới, chính sách thu đổi định hướng theo giá thị trường tại thời điểm 
giao dịch, thay cho tỷ lệ cố định trên giá trị hóa đơn, nhằm hạn chế tiếp tục tích lũy 
nghĩa vụ tiềm tàng trong tương lai theo cơ chế cũ.

Phương án bổ sung vốn và tái cơ cấu hoạt động kinh doanh 
— Đại hội thông qua phương án chào bán tối đa 550 triệu cổ phiếu. Nguồn vốn dự kiến 
phân bổ 60% cho kế hoạch kinh doanh gắn với điều chỉnh chính sách thu đổi, 20% bổ 
sung vốn lưu động và phần còn lại bổ sung vốn lưu động và/hoặc trả vay ngắn hạn. 
Nhu cầu vốn Q4/2026 và Q1/2027 lần lượt khoảng 3,100 tỷ VND và 2,800 tỷ VND. Giá và 
khối lượng phát hành thực tế chưa được xác định tại đại hội.  
— Doanh nghiệp dự kiến đóng 25–30 cửa hàng và mở khoảng 10 cửa hàng (có chọn lọc) 
trong năm 2026, tương ứng giảm ròng 15–20 cửa hàng; đồng thời đặt mục tiêu giảm 
20–30% chi phí nhân sự. Cổ tức tiền mặt năm 2026 được điều chỉnh về 0%; cổ tức 2025 
về 10% mệnh giá và đã chi trả. 
— 100% mẫu kim cương được AIGS và Vinacontrol kiểm định độc lập là kim cương thiên 
nhiên, phù hợp thông số 4C. PNJ dự kiến trang bị máy kiểm định tại các cửa hàng trọng 
điểm để khách hàng kiểm tra trực tiếp.

Đánh giá của KBSV 
— KBSV cho rằng huy động vốn từ phát hành riêng lẻ là phương án phù hợp ở thời điểm 
hiện tại trong bối cảnh lãi suất tăng cao, giúp PNJ cân đối thanh khoản và duy trì hoạt 
động liên tục. Tuy nhiên, với khối lượng phát hành lớn và chưa có phương án chi tiết, 
doanh nghiệp sẽ cần thêm thời gian để hoàn thành phương án phát hành và cân đối 
giữa lợi ích của cổ đông và doanh nghiệp.  
— Nhu cầu yếu tại Trung Quốc và cạnh tranh từ kim cương nuôi cấy giá thấp tiếp tục gây 
áp lực lên giá kim cương tự nhiên, khiến PNJ gặp khó khăn trong việc tiêu thụ tồn kho 
nhóm kim cương  và gia tăng tổn thất khi mua lại theo chính sách cũ. Thực tế, tỷ trọng 
kim cương và trang sức kim cương trong tổng tồn kho tăng từ 41% cuối Q2/2026 lên 
52% cuối Q3/2026, với giá trị ước khoảng 9,000 tỷ VND. KBSV cho rằng cần theo dõi (1) 
nhu cầu thị trường trong giai đoạn cuối năm; (2) tốc độ giảm tồn kho và dòng tiền thu 
về từ bán hàng; và (3) giá trị khách hàng bán lại. Diễn biến các yếu tố này sẽ quyết định 
khả năng thu hồi vốn, mức độ phục hồi biên lợi nhuận và nhu cầu trích lập bổ sung 
trong quá trình xử lý. 
— Ước tính bán niên sử dụng tỷ lệ tổn thất 20.3% đối với hàng bán trong giai đoạn 2020–
2026 dự kiến được thu mua lại, thấp hơn mức 28.9% trên các giao dịch mua lại thực tế 
từ 01/07–03/09/2026. Vì vậy, bên cạnh giả định thu đổi 80%, mức tổn thất khi xử lý 
hàng mua lại là yếu tố quan trọng cần theo dõi. Việc chuyển hạch toán dự phòng từ 
SG&A sang giá vốn tại kỳ soát xét bán niên cũng cho thấy áp lực biên lợi nhuận bị co 
hẹp trong quá trình tái cấu trúc về mặt kế toán.

Nvidia 與  Tesla 聯⼿注資三星電機，除了試圖打破  ABF 載板⿓頭⻑
期短缺及漲價壟斷，是否也為了採⽤三星 2nm 晶圓代⼯鋪路 ?
ANDREW LU ON GLOBAL SEMI/TECHS

OCT 23 ∙ PAID
READ IN APP
What’s new?
2026 年  10 ⽉  22 ⽇⾄  23 ⽇，路透社（ Reuters ）、南韓《每⽇經濟新聞》
（ Maeil Business Newspaper ）與多家科技產業媒體同步披露：全球  AI 晶
⽚霸主輝達（ NVIDIA ）與電動⾞及具⾝智能巨頭特斯拉（ Tesla ），聯⼿向南
韓三星電機（ Samsung Electro-Mechanics ）注資約  28 億美元（折合約
3.7 兆韓元），⽤於其在南韓世宗市（ Sejong ）⼯廠⼤規模新建專⽤⾼階覆晶
球柵陣列（ FC-BGA / ABF Substract ）半導體封裝載板產線。此前幾天（ 10
⽉  19 ⽇），三星電機已於官⽅聲明中正式確認與全球⼤型企業達成戰略夥伴協
議，將⾃  2027 年 1 ⽉ 1 ⽇起正式向⼤客戶供應⾼階半導體封裝載板（三星电机
与全球⼤型企业签订 “ 存储器半导体⽤封装基板 ” ⻓期供应合同（ LTA ））。報導

指出，在  28 億美元注資總承諾中，輝達預計出資約  2.6 兆韓元，特斯拉出資
約  1.1 兆韓元。這筆巨額資⾦旨在為輝達下⼀代  Blackwell Ultra GB300
NVL72 超級計算叢集、 Rubin 架構  GPU ，以及特斯拉即將量產的  HW5 ⾃動
駕駛⾞載晶⽚與  Optimus 機器⼈神經網絡晶⽚，鎖定最為緊缺的⾼層數、⼤
⾯積先進封裝載板產能。

特斯拉兩⼤全球最⼤  AI 算⼒買家的巨額資本與⻑約鎖定，使其⼀舉打破傳統
⽇系（ Ibiden, Shinko) 與台系廠商 ( 欣興，南電）的⻑期壟斷，躍升為全球
AI 載板⼀線核⼼主⼒。
ABF 味之素積層膜材料商（⽇本味之素  Ajinomoto ）與載板設備商：⼤⾯
積  FC-BGA 載板層數已⾼達  20 層以上，良率爬坡極耗材料，直接帶動上游特

Negatively aﬀect what supply chain vendors and
explains why?
傳統載板雙寡頭（⽇本揖斐電  Ibiden 、新光電氣  Shinko ）及台系傳統載板
廠（欣興、南電）：輝達與特斯拉主動扶持三星電機建⽴新載板產能，短期可

能看不出來，但⻑期將削弱現有⽇台供應商過去兩年在⾼階載板定價上的絕對
話語權與超額利潤率，並改變市場份額。以 28 億美元投資來看，雖然僅佔 4%
的三星電機市值，但確明顯超過  Ibiden 及欣興近 10-15 億美元的年度資本開
⽀。
晶圓代⼯⿓頭台積電及封測產業鏈：從 Nvidia 及 Tesla 在 HBM DRAM ， DDR
DRAM ， NAND 與三星電⼦的採購關係，擴⼤到三星電機  ABF 載板產能擴充
的注資，我們不排除  Nvidia 及 Tesla 將進⼀步採⽤三星電⼦ 2nm （近期良率
有明顯改善）晶圓代⼯製程及封測技術，讓三星集團進⾏  AI 訓練及推理晶⽚
製造整合服務來搶台廠份額。
未獲得載板保證的⼆線  ASIC 晶⽚設計公司：頂級載板產能被巨頭包攬，使資
⾦鏈較弱的中⼩  AI 晶⽚公司⾯臨載板交期延⻑⾄  50 週以上的斷鏈危機。
Our outside the box non-consensus thinking
特斯拉參投載板是「實體  AI 製造去台化 / 去⽇化」的戰略預演：市場多將注意

⼒集中於輝達，但特斯拉⾸次拿出  1.1 兆韓元直投載板廠更具震撼性。這證明
⾺斯克已深刻意識到 ——HW5 晶⽚與百萬台  Optimus 的真正死⽳不僅僅是
晶圓代⼯產能短缺，⽽也包括 ABF 封裝載板⾯積及厚度激增引發的良率瓶頸。
特斯拉直接以⾞企⾝分下場穿透供應鏈投資底層零組件，正在重構半導體採購
範式。
「晶⽚⾯積膨脹」倒逼載板成為半導體新護城河：隨著晶⽚尺⼨光罩極限朝
10x 以上突破（ Reticle Limit ），單顆  GB300 或  HW5 載板尺⼨已超過
100mm x 100mm ，相當於⼀塊⼩型主機板。載板製造已從傳統  PCB 加⼯
轉變為「類晶圓級微影⼯程」，載板產能的鎖定甚⾄⽐搶奪台積電  CoWoS 配
額更具決定性。

### Page 3

Our advises to investors
我們建議投資⼈關注三星電機（ Samsung Electro-Mechanics ）及其核⼼材
料設備供應商在產業份額的變化，尤其是在輝達與特斯拉雙重認證下，其⾼階
載板營收佔⽐將在未來兩年由不⾜  15% 是否能飆升⾄  30% 以上，迎來估值
重估。適度獲利了結估值處於歷史⾼點的傳統純載板標的，防範  2027 年三星
電機新產能開出後，⾏業整體⽑利率⾯臨均值回歸的競爭壓⼒。三星電機產線
在⼤⾯積  FC-BGA 的翹曲控制（ Warpage Control ）與熱膨脹良率爬坡仍值
得觀察。

• The Westpac–DataX Card Tracker
Index* has edged down slightly since
late September, slipping 0.9pts to 160.6
in the week ended October 17. Despite
this, growth in activity continues to track
a robust pace with quarterly momentum

running at close to 2%qtr and annual
growth at 6%yr.
• Some of this reflects revisions. Initial
figures on card activity can see changes
in subsequent weeks due to information
lags. While these are usually small,
the cumulative impact can sometimes
be material. In this case, revisions have
lifted the index in late September by
2.5pts and the 4wk trend growth rate at
the time from 0.2% to 0.6%. We will be
back-testing regular revisions to assess
whether there is a systematic bias that

can be adjusted for in future.
• The revised profile shows quarterly
growth accelerating steadily since
June, posting a 1.9%qtr gain for the
September quarter and tracking a
2.1%qtr pace in the latest week. This is
back near the robust pace seen in late
2024 and early 2025.
• The monthly and weekly reads show
a softer 0.2%mth rise in August was
followed by a solid 0.8%mth gain in
September that has largely carried into
the first two weeks of October (the latest

observation tracking a 0.4%mth pace).
• The detail shows gains are not just
due to higher fuel prices with strong
growth in discretionary services and
international transactions over the last
quarter and steady growth in essential
services as well. To the extent that the
weekly and monthly data is showing
some renewed squeeze from higher fuel
prices, this is showing through most
clearly in discretionary goods, which
have seen activity decline over the last

six weeks, with discretionary services
still posting positive, albeit slower
growth. Quarterly growth in non-fuel
card activity is tracking around 1.4%qtr.
• Official September quarter figures on
inflation and household spending due
in the coming weeks should confirm the
spending momentum and shed more
light on the extent to which it is due to
prices or volumes.

running at close to 2%qtr ...”

1. Westpac–DataX Card Tracker Index*
Card activity showing robust growth but with a mixed start to October
The Westpac–DataX Card Tracker presents indicators based on the millions of
credit and debit card transactions processed by Westpac every day. The measures
are a timely guide to shifts in spending. See p 10 for a detailed explanation.

3
• Chart 2 shows how quarterly growth
in the Westpac–DataX Card Tracker
Index compares to growth in nominal
consumption as reported in the national
accounts. Growth has shown a clear
acceleration since late June, lifting to
1.9%qtr in the September quarter and

running at 2.1%qtr in the latest week.
Weekly reads in early October have been
the strongest since July 2025.
• Only some of this reflects a price-
driven rise in fuel spend. The unwind of
temporary fuel excise tax cuts in August
and more recent surge in global energy
prices has seen local pump prices push
back above $2.40/litre locally, up from
$1.56/litre in late June.
• Excluding fuel, quarterly growth in card
activity has moderated, but only slightly,

from 1.9%qtr in August to 1.8% in
September with the latest week tracking
a 1.4% quarterly pace. The impact is
more pronounced in monthly terms with
a –0.4%mth decline in August, muted
+0.2% gain in September and subdued
+0.1% monthly pace in the latest week
(c.f. total: –0.4%; +0.2% and +0.1%).
• The 1.9%qtr rise in nominal terms and an
expected price rise of 1.1%qtr point to a
real, inflation-adjusted gain of 0.8%qtr,
in line with Westpac’s forecasts. ABS Q3

CPI figures are due shortly after we go to
press with the Q3 household spending
indicator due Nov 4 and GDP due Dec 2.

• Chart 4 shows quarterly growth in card

• The contribution from essential goods
captures the effect of fuel price swings.

have been the main driver of growth

has essentially stalled flat. In quarterly

at 2.0%qtr and discretionary goods much

of NSW and Vic account for nearly two

cleaning often result in shifts to historical
estimates. These are typically small but
can be have a material cumulative effect.

revision: for the last week of September, it
lifts the WCTI 2.5pts and the 4wk trend
growth rate from 0.2% to 0.6%, removing
what looked to be a slowing in momentum
at the time. We will be investigating this

issue more closely in coming weeks to see
whether there is a systematic bias that
can be adjusted for in the future.

WESTPAC ECONOMICS 5
• Our MHSI and retail card indexes 
are composite measures based on 
transactions in categories that are in 
scope for the ABS monthly household 
spending indicator (MHSI) and retail 
sales estimates (which have been based 
on the MHSI detail since the ABS retail 
survey was discontinued in June 2025). 
• After under-performing the broader 
WCTI in Q2, the MHSI index has moved 
more in line in Q3, posting growth of 
1.6%qtr with the latest week running  at 
2%qtr. The retail card index has been 
more subdued, up 1.1%qtr in Q3, growth 
running at just 0.4%qtr in the latest week.
• The ABS MHSI has been firmer than 
our index since June, mainly reflecting 
strong gains in vehicle sales which are 
not incorporated into our index. Vehicle 
sales appear to have been relatively flat in 
recent months, our estimates suggesting 
the official measure, due Nov 4, should 
show quarterly growth more in line with 
our indicator (Chart 7).
• The more granular detail on the retail 
card index shows non-food retail 
contracting materially over the last four 
weeks, consistent with the weakness 
being observed across discretionary 
goods categories.

WESTPAC ECONOMICS 6
• The Westpac Card Tracker draws on
a rich dataset that sheds light on many
aspects of consumer demand. In some
cases, it can also reveal some nuances
about the way spend is captured – in
official statistics, industry measures and
the card data itself. Vehicles provide a

particularly good example of this.
• Vehicle spend has long been a
bellwether for the wider consumer cycle.
As a large discretionary expenditure, it is
more sensitive to interest rate changes,
‘wealth effects’ and sentiment shifts,
particularly around job security. Given
this, ABS measures and industry figures
on vehicle sales have delivered material
upside surprises over the last year.
Vehicle purchases recorded a remarkable
10.3%qtr surge in the June quarter to be

up 7.3%yr with industry figures showing
vehicle sales holding around these levels
in the September quarter. Even allowing
‘extenuating circumstances’ driving
strong demand for EVs, the results seem
to fly in the face of wider interest rate,
cost-of-living and sentiment narratives.
• The card tracker data allows for a deeper
dive. Note that credit and debit cards are
not often used to purchase vehicles with
account transfers or financing typically

used for the main purchase instead.
• However, cards are used for up-front
payments. This shows through in
payment sizes, which are in the hundreds
of dollars (average $750) not the tens of
thousands. As such, card activity should
still give some guide, particularly around
sales volumes.
• Chart 8 shows how transactions have
tracked and highlight another important
point. Most of the data in this space
focuses on new vehicles (for the ABS
this is because used sales are a transfer

between households that ‘cancels out’ for
the sector as a whole). However, industry
estimates show used sales are both more
common and have been tracking a weaker
profile – the card data suggests used sales
are down at least 3%yr (finer estimates
putting the figure closer to 8%yr).
• Age-groups offer another insightful lens.
Activity has been much firmer across
older age groups, particularly in the ‘new
vehicle’ segment where they are more
prominent (accounting for 42% of all

activity) and activity is up on a year ago.
This group has notably stronger balance
sheets and is low exposure to interest
rate rises. In contrast, vehicle spend has
been contracting materially across other
age-groups, particularly used car spend
by the ‘mortgage belt’ 35-54 year olds.
Vehicle-related card activity highlights diverging trends

WESTPAC ECONOMICS 10
About the Westpac card data indicators
The indicators presented in this report are based on the millions of credit and debit card
transactions processed every day. Transactions covering over ten million merchants are
classified into over 700 categories. These are in turn grouped into higher level aggregates

that provide a timely guide to wider economic trends.
The main focus of these indicators is consumer spending. Where possible, we have sought
to exclude ‘non spending’ transactions such as: money transfers; tax payments; loan
repayments; charitable donations; and superannuation contributions.
It should also be noted that these indicators will also be affected by shifts between card and
non-card transactions. This was a significant factor during the COVID–19 pandemic – health

concerns about the use of physical cash leading to significantly higher use of cards vs cash,
particularly where contact–less transactions are available. Transaction flows also include
reversals/refunds which were also a significant phenomenon during the onset of COVID,
especially in areas such as travel.
All transaction data is compiled at a highly aggregated level so that individual customer or
merchant data is never revealed.
Index construction

The key metrics used in this report are indexes of spending–related card activity where the
base of 100 is average activity in 2019. As an example, if transaction flows are 5% above their
average level in 2019, the index read for the period is 105. If flows in a subsequent period are
8% above the average level in 2019, the index read for this period is 108. Growth between the
two periods can be calculated simply as the change between the two index reads, i.e. 2.9%.

All measures are adjusted for regular seasonality. Weekly estimates are generated using
the US Bureau of Labor’s MoveReg weekly seasonal adjustment program. Note that in some
cases, high levels of volatility during the COVID period mean it is not possible to produce
seasonally adjusted estimates for some historical periods.
Also, note that previous versions of this report used different approaches to seasonal
adjustment and measurement more generally. This means Index reads are sometimes not

directly comparable. See the ‘About the Westpac card data indicators’ sections from earlier
reports to more detail.
Classifications
Note that the measures used for card data and in this report do not align completely with
the those used in official ABS statistics, including the ABS household spending indicator,
ABS retail trade survey and ABS estimate of consumer spending published in the national
accounts. There are a range of differences including around both coverage and classification.

As such, the card data should be treated as broadly indicative.
The transaction data is grouped into 26 categories that are then combined into four main as
follows:
Discretionary goods: alcoholic beverages, tobacco, clothing & footwear, furnishings &
household equipment, household appliances, vehicle–related, recreational & cultural goods,
newspaper, books & stationery, and other personal effects.
Discretionary services: transport services (part), recreational & cultural services, gambling,

catering services, accommodation services, other personal care, insurance & financial
services, other services.
Essential goods: food & non alcoholic beverages, medical products, appliances & equipment,
and operation of personal transport equipment.
Essential services: electricity, gas & other fuels, health services, transport services (part),
communications and education.
The report also uses two additional classifications:
MHSI/Retail: based on the extent to which categories cover activity that is in scope for the

ABS monthly household spending indicator and ABS retail trade survey.

1 
PCB 產業 – TPCA Show 2026 直擊 
規格持續提升帶動 PCB、CCL 材料、技術混合化 
What’s New – CCL M10、M11 規格正式問世 
AI 伺服器與高階應用相關需求，推動 PCB 產業整體供應鏈朝更高階持續升級，其中在高頻
高速 CCL 方面，台光電作為產業領頭羊，推出了最新 M10 系列材料 EM-898K4，搭配升級
版石英布，並結合自主研發之 HC+PPE 樹脂配方；台燿除石英布材料解方 TU-963Q 外，更
推出 TU-963F / TU-973F 等採用無玻纖布之 RCC 結構解方 ， 而聯茂亦具備 IT-999GSE3/ IT-
Q10GSE4 等相對應之產品，多項產品的演進與推出，皆在強化自身高速運算與高頻領域的
競爭力。顯示整體市場正朝向高頻、高速與高可靠度的材料新時代邁進。 
Trends/Outlook – 材料升級趨勢不變 
不論是在上游 CCL 材料中因追求電性表現，而重新受到討論的 PTFE 樹酯以及混壓玻纖方
案，抑或是載板為求維持結構剛性，Multi-Layer Core 的增加以及 Glass Core 的方案提出，
以及 PCB 廠商為滿足伺服器規格需求，PCB 板材將更多採用到 HLC 與 HDI 的混合製程，以
上雖然應用與技術大不相同，但皆是受到產品規格與需求不斷推升之下，而持續推動的階解
決方案，並帶動材料升級趨勢不變。 
Investment Summary 
1. 研究部認為製程良率、成本管控上將會是材料選用之關鍵，且材料定案時程尚需時
間，惟整體 CCL 產業材料持續升級趨勢不變下，維持看好台光電(2383 TT) 、台燿
(6274 TT)、聯茂(6213 TT)以及上游銅箔廠商金居(8358 TT)。 
2. 看好 PCB 板廠更多採用到 HLC 與 HDI 的混合製程以及 mSAP 製程； 且持續看好載
板廠商受益晶片尺寸提升 ，Multi-Layer Core 等技術持續提升 ， 帶動產品單價走揚，
包括臻鼎-KY(4958 TT)、華通(2313 TT)、欣興(3037 TT)、景碩(3089 TT)。

投資 顧問 
產業報告 
 2026/10/27

2 
PCB 異質整合邁向半導體化新紀元 
TPCA Show 2026 於 10 月 20 日在台北南港展覽館一館正式開展。今年展會以Energy-Efficient AI: 
From Computing to Systems and Applications為主題，延續節能 AI 之主軸，但由過去聚焦 AI 雲
端、邊緣應用裝置，走向從晶片運算、系統集應用的完整層次。本次產會內容涵蓋了涵蓋載板與高階
電路板製造、高質新材料與化學品、乾濕製程設備、智動化解決方案、檢測設備、電子構裝、 SMT
智慧生產及綠色科技， 並且首度新增先進封裝主題。本次展會匯聚超過 680 家國際知名品牌與 1,840
個展位，三天展期吸引數萬名國際買家參與，現場人潮與產業熱度皆持續創歷年新高。

規格持續提升，PCB 各應用皆出現混合材料、技術解方 
本次展會中持續聚焦於因應 AI 算力以及高速傳輸之規格提升。因 AI 資料中心持續提升運算效能，
算力需求持續大幅提升，隨著晶片整合規模擴大、功耗增加，PCB/CCL 以及 IC 載板規格持續朝向
大面積、多層數擴張，同時在訊號、電性傳輸和膨脹控制表現上更為要求。另外，本次展會中我們
也發現，不論是在上游 CCL 材料中因追求電性表現，而重新受到討論的 PTFE 樹酯以及混壓玻纖方
案，抑或是載板為求維持結構剛性，Multi-Layer Core 的增加以及 Glass Core 的方案提出，以及
PCB 廠商為滿足伺服器規格需求，PCB 板材將更多採用到 HLC 與 HDI 的混合製程，以上雖然應用
與技術大不相同，但皆是受到產品規格與需求不斷推升之下，而持續推動的高階解決方案。 
圖 1. 載板 Glass Core 的方案提出/ HLC 與 HDI 的混合製程示意圖 
資料來源：TPCA、欣興，國泰證期研究部彙整 
我們認為 CCL 規格持續升級的趨勢並不會改變。台光電身為產業龍頭，除已進入量產的 M9 系列材
料 EM-896K3 外，亦推出了最新 M10 系列材料 EM-898K4，搭配升級版石英布，並結合自主研發
之 HC+PPE 樹脂配方；台燿除石英布材料解方 TU-963Q 外，更推出TU-963F / TU-973F 等採用無
玻纖布之 RCC 結構解方，而聯茂亦具備 IT-999GSE3/ IT-Q10GSE4 等相對應之產品，多項產品的
演進與推出，皆在強化自身高速運算與高頻領域的競爭力。預期進入至 27 年，將會有更多高階 AI
伺服器與 1.6T 交換器的 CCL 材料將開始邁入 M8+/M9 世代，而隨一般伺服器由 PCIe 5.0 跨入至
6.0，M7 等級 CCL 亦將成為標準配置，另外更多特殊應用場景如衛星板材、光模塊等，隨著技術的
成長與需求提升之下，亦將往 M7/M8 等級 CCL 進行迭代，整體市場正朝向高頻、高速與高可靠度
的材料新時代邁進。目前於最高規格之 CCL 解方出現分歧，Q-Glass 石英布與 PTFE 等無布方案皆
受到高度討論，惟研究部認為製程良率、成本管控上將會是材料選用、量產之關鍵，且材料定案時
程仍尚需要一些時間，惟整體 CCL 產業材料持續升級趨勢不變下，在規格和用量上皆持續向上。 
而在 IC 載板部分，高階載板亦持續向大尺寸、多層數趨勢邁進，同時因尺寸大幅提升之下，多核心
層與玻璃核心層等概念已被相繼提出 ， 加上部分載板以內部整合元件作為設計之下 ，IC 載板於技術 、
產能上將面臨更大的挑戰。

### Page 3

投資 顧問 
產業報告 
 2026/10/27

3 
台光電(2383 TT) 
台光電子材料股份有限公司成立於 1992 年，初期主要供應 FR-4 銅箔基板與膠片；並於 2013 年，
成為全球最大綠色環保材(無鹵素)基板供應商，迄今仍在市場保持領先地位。靠著專業的研發團隊，
台光持續開發出各種優良的綠色環保材新產品，從 Mid. Loss、 Low Loss、Very Low Loss、Ultra 
low loss 到 Extreme Low Loss 等不同等級材料，並符合各種高精密先進 PCB 技術，如 Anylayer、
mSAP、IC 載板、超高層板、高速傳輸及高頻產品等各應用場域之需求，並獲得眾多客戶肯定。 
秉持著創造價值的信念，致力於持續的技術創新與改善，台光電子已獲得全球 250 項以上的專利，
並於智慧型手機、AI 人工智慧、超級電腦、雲端資料中心、5G 網路、電動車及自動駕駛等應用領域
取得技術領先地位。

圖 2. 台光電各主要終端應用產品 
資料來源：台光電，國泰證期研究部彙整

TPCA 參訪紀要 
1. Mobile device 材料包括 390、526、528、530 及 720，趨勢為 XY CTE、Z CTE 越來越低，以
提高尺寸安定性；Tg 越來越高，Dk、Df 越來越低。主因是 AI 晶片從邊緣端到雲端，pitch 密
度越來越高，對尺寸精準度的要求提升。 
2. 390 廣泛用於 NB、平板及 AI PC。526 用於手機，也大量用於記憶體模組，DDR4 轉 DDR5、
DDR6 後被業界廣泛採用。兩支材料是目前主要的出貨來源。528 為下一代材料，Dk 較低，目
前手機主要 OEM 正在評估。S530 及 720 用於 Core 材或 Hybrid 材料，用來支撐機械強度與
CTE。

### Page 4

投資 顧問 
產業報告 
 2026/10/27

4 
3. 載板材料方面，S526 物性與 526 相同，但在潔淨度較高的環境生產，部分到 Class 1000 。公
司在各廠設置 Substrate Production Line（SPT Line） ，崑山已有一定產能，台灣新建廠房及中
山也有產能，黃石或檳城亦有規劃。 
4. S536K 用於光模組，CTE 及 Z 軸 CTE 低於 S526，Dk 表現更好，可將DSP 與 Connector 置於
同一塊板子上，已透過 1.6T 光模組，即將量產。 
5. S570、S570T、S572T 對標日系大廠產品。S570 CTE 約 4 至 6 ppm，S570T 因加入 T-glass，
XY CTE 約 2 至 3 ppm，S572T 的 XY 及 Z 軸 CTE 更低，楊氏係數也越來越高。載板材料重點
在 CTE 與尺寸安定性，電性要求次之。國外大廠已開始使用，特別是穿戴裝置。 
6. S572T 已被某 CSP 的 AI ASIC 採用，4Q26 在 AI 晶片端量產，公司視為里程碑，後續將有更
多 CSP 及 CPU 大廠採用。此系列作為Core 層與 ABF 搭配，另一系列與BT 系列搭配，兩者物
性與競品相當。 
7. 高速材方面，892K 為 M8 標配，公司出貨已達百萬張以上等級，被各 CSP 廣泛採用；896K3
為 M9 材料，使用 Q-glass；898K4 為 M10 材料，為因應前端 AI 公司需求開發。 
8. 基礎建設板對 XY CTE 的要求已超越 HDI， 例如892K3 約 4 至 6 ppm、898K4 約 6 至 8 ppm，
主因晶片 pitch 越來越密。同時伺服器板尺寸大，對低損耗要求也比 HDI 更高，892K 以上 Dk
皆低於 3（約 2.7 至 2.8），898K4 的 Df 約萬分之四。公司強調能同時兼顧電性與尺寸安定性，
是三十年 HDI 研發累積的差異化優勢。 
9. 物理 AI 方面，370Z 為 EV ADAS、800V 高壓充電及人形機器人運算的標配，Tg、CTE 及 Dk
表現普通，但800V 以內的抗高壓表現特別好。A50、A56K 用於次世代 ADAS 及人形機器人大
腦，Dk 分別約 3.4、3.8，優於 370Z，目前已開始做 Design。A70K 用於 77 至 79 GHz 雷達。
A30 為更進階的抗高壓材料，對應 1,000V 以上至 1,500V 甚至 3,000V。 
10. 工業應用以 ATE 為代表，Probe card 等板層數比 CSP 板更高，需兼顧尺寸安定性與 Dk、Df，
使用 890K、892K 及 896K3。客戶群與資料中心不同，屬 Niche 市場。 
11. M10 預計 2027 年量產；S572T 4Q26 量產後，將有更多 CSP 及 CPU 大廠採用，公司預期
2027 年在載板市場大有斬獲。

聯茂(6213 TT) 
聯茂電子股份有限公司成立於 1997 年，總部位於台灣新竹縣，為台灣銅箔基板供應商之一。主要產
品包括銅箔基板、膠片、多層壓合基板專業代工、散熱材料，於台灣、中國、泰國皆設有生產據點。
主要產品應用於 5G 通信設備、雲端資料中心、車用電子、智慧型手機及消費性電子等領域。 
作為印刷電路板基礎材料的供應鏈之一，聯茂一向全心投入，產品特性之表現與成本之競爭力一向
是其產品設計之主要推力，自行研發之優勢，帶領公司進入世界級領導品牌之林，尤其是無鉛製程
時代所需之無鹵，高 Tg 高頻之特殊材料，聯茂提供全方位解決方案、硬板、軟板、軟硬結合板膠片
等一站到位之服務。
